To address these challenges, we propose Aes3D, the first systematic framework for assessing the aesthetics of 3D neural rendering scenes. Aes3D includes Aesthetic3D, the first dataset dedicated to 3D scene aesthetic assessment, built on our proposed annotation strategy for 3D scene aesthetics. In addition, we present Aes3DGSNet, a lightweight model that directly predicts scene-level aesthetic scores from 3DGS representations. Notably, our model operates solely on 3D Gaussian primitives, eliminating the need for rendering multi-view images and thus reducing computational cost and hardware requirements. Through aesthetics-supervised learning on multi-view 3DGS scene representations, Aes3DGSNet effectively captures high-level aesthetic cues and accurately regresses aesthetic scores. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance while maintaining a lightweight design, establishing a new benchmark for 3D scene aesthetic assessment. Code and datasets will be made available in a future version.

</details>


### [148] [InfoGeo: Information-Theoretic Object-Centric Learning for Cross-View Generalizable UAV Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07099)
*Hongyang Zhang,Maonnan Wang,Ziyao Wang,Hongrui Yin,Man On Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InfoGeo框架，利用信息瓶颈和物体中心学习，通过最大化跨视角不变信息和最小化视角特定噪声，显著提升跨视角地理定位在复杂场景下的鲁棒性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法依赖全局特征对齐，但在区域纹理变化、天气条件和无人机视角下密集细粒度物体造成的视觉杂乱中表现不佳，鲁棒性和泛化性不足。

Method: 借鉴物体中心学习（OCL），提出InfoGeo信息论框架。将优化重构为信息瓶颈过程：(i) 通过对齐跨视角的物体中心结构关系最大化视角不变信息；(ii) 通过跨视角知识约束最小化视角特定噪声信号。

Result: 在多个基准和挑战性场景下的广泛评估表明，InfoGeo显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: InfoGeo通过信息瓶颈和物体中心结构对齐，有效解决了跨视角地理定位中的领域偏移和视觉杂乱问题，提升了模型在复杂环境下的定位精度和泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization (CVGL) is fundamental for precise localization and navigation in GPS-denied environments, aiming to match ground or UAV imagery with satellite views. Existing approaches often rely on global feature alignment, but they suffer from substantial domain shifts induced by varying regional textures and weather conditions. This issue becomes even more pronounced in UAV-based scenarios, where the broader perspective inevitably introduces dense, fine-grained objects, creating significant visual clutter. To address this, we draw inspiration from Object-Centric Learning (OCL) and propose InfoGeo, an information-theoretic framework designed to enhance robustness and generalization. InfoGeo reformulates the optimization as an information bottleneck process with two core objectives: (i) maximizing view-invariant information by aligning the object-centric structural relations across views, and (ii) minimizing view-specific noisy signals through cross-view knowledge constraints. Extensive evaluations across diverse benchmarks and challenging scenarios demonstrate that InfoGeo significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [149] [CaC: Advancing Video Reward Models via Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Concentrating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11723)
*Jiyuan Wang,Huan Ouyang,Jiuzhou Lin,Chunyu Lin,Dewen Fan,Boheng Zhang,Haonan Fan,Fei Zuo,Jia Sun,Huaiqing Wang,Honglie Wang,Yiyang Fan,Zhenlong Yuan,Zijun Li,Yongrui Heng,Guosheng Lin,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CaC（Concentrate and Concentrate）模型，一种基于视觉语言模型的粗到细异常奖励模型。通过全局时间扫描定位异常时间窗口，然后在局部区间内进行细粒度空间定位，最后通过结构化时空思维链推理得出稳健判断。该模型构建了首个大规模生成视频异常数据集（含逐帧边界框、时间异常窗口和细粒度归因标签），并设计了三阶段渐进训练范式，包括单/多帧监督微调、两轮GRPO强化学习及时空IoU奖励。实验表明，CaC在细粒度异常基准上提升25.7%准确率，作为奖励信号可减少11.7%生成视频异常并提升整体视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测方法在检测微妙、细粒度异常时存在不足，且缺乏能够同时进行时空定位和可解释推理的模型。作者旨在构建一个能够从粗到细精准定位视频异常、并具备可解释推理能力的奖励模型，以提升视频异常检测和生成质量。

Method: 1) 粗到细推理框架：全局时间扫描→局部空间定位→结构化时空CoT推理；2) 构建首个大规模生成视频异常数据集（含逐帧边界框、时间窗口、归因标签）；3) 三阶段渐进训练：单帧SFT→多帧SFT→两轮GRPO强化学习；4) 引入Temporal IoU和Spatial IoU奖励监督中间定位过程。

Result: 细粒度异常基准上准确率提升25.7%；作为奖励信号时，生成视频异常减少11.7%，整体视频质量提升。

Conclusion: CaC能够稳定聚焦于细微异常，通过结构化时空推理提升检测准确性和可解释性，同时可作为有效奖励信号改善视频生成质量，验证了粗到细异常检测范式的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Concentrate and Concentrate (CaC), a coarse-to-fine anomaly reward model based on Vision-Language Models. During inference, it first conducts a global temporal scan to anchor anomalous time windows, then performs fine-grained spatial grounding within the localized interval, and finally derives robust judgments via structured spatiotemporal Chain-of-Thought reasoning. To equip the model with these capabilities, we construct the first large-scale generated video anomaly dataset with per-frame bounding-box annotations, temporal anomaly windows, and fine-grained attribution labels. Building on this dataset, we design a three-stage progressive training paradigm. The model initially learns spatial and temporal anchoring through single- and multi-frame supervised fine-tuning, and then is optimized by a reinforcement learning strategy based on two-turn Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Beyond conventional accuracy rewards, we introduce Temporal and Spatial IoU rewards to supervise the intermediate localization process, effectively guiding the model toward more grounded and interpretable spatiotemporal reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CaC can stably concentrate on subtle anomalies, achieving a 25.7% accuracy improvement on fine-grained anomaly benchmarks and, when used as a reward signal, CaC reduces generated-video anomalies by 11.7% while improving overall video quality.

</details>


### [150] [Diagnosing and Correcting Concept Omission in Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14270)
*Kanghyun Baek,Jaihyun Lew,Chaehun Shin,Jungbeom Lee,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遗漏信号干预（OSI）的方法，通过放大文本嵌入中的"遗漏信号"来缓解多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）在文本到图像生成中的概念遗漏问题，实验证明在FLUX.1-Dev和SD3.5-Medium上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer在文本到图像生成中经常出现概念遗漏（指定的物体或属性未出现在生成图像中），需要解决这一问题。

Method: 通过线性探测（linear probing）文本token，发现文本嵌入中存在表征目标概念缺失的"遗漏信号"；提出遗漏信号干预（OSI）方法，放大该信号以主动催化缺失概念的生成。

Result: 在FLUX.1-Dev和SD3.5-Medium上，OSI显著缓解了概念遗漏问题，即使在极端场景下也有效。

Conclusion: 放大文本嵌入中的遗漏信号是缓解多模态扩散Transformer概念遗漏问题的有效方法。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) have achieved remarkable progress in text-to-image generation, yet they frequently suffer from concept omission, where specified objects or attributes fail to emerge in the generated image. By performing linear probing on text tokens, we demonstrate that text embeddings can distinguish a characteristic `omission signal' representing the absence of target concepts. Leveraging this insight, we propose Omission Signal Intervention (OSI), which amplifies the omission signal to actively catalyze the generation of missing concepts. Comprehensive experiments on FLUX.1-Dev and SD3.5-Medium demonstrate that OSI significantly alleviates concept omission even in extreme scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [GHOST: Geometry-Hierarchical Online Streaming Token Eviction for Efficient 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15852)
*Leyang Chen,Junyi Wu,Zhiteng Li,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHOST是一个无需训练的KV缓存管理框架，通过利用模型自身的3D几何输出来在线逐出冗余token，在长单目视频流式3D重建中，将KV缓存削减近一半，推理速度提升1.75倍，同时保持优异的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法中，KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成严重内存瓶颈。剪枝方法要么固定锚帧导致质量下降，要么使用与3D结构无关的注意力分数启发式，无法保留几何上有价值的token。

Method: GHOST包含三个相互增强的创新：(1) 层次化双级重要性评分方案；(2) 保护特殊token不被逐出的特权机制；(3) 余弦相似度引导的逐层预算分配。

Result: 在多个基准上，GHOST将KV缓存削减近一半，推理速度相比最先进方法提升1.75倍，同时保持优异的3D重建质量。

Conclusion: GHOST通过利用模型自身3D几何输出指导token逐出，有效解决了流式3D重建中KV缓存内存瓶颈问题，是一种高效且无需额外训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction from long monocular video sequences requires maintaining a key-value (KV) cache that grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck. Existing approaches either truncate the cache to a fixed set of anchor frames, leading to reconstruction quality degradation, or rely on attention-score heuristics that are agnostic to 3D scene structure, failing to preserve geometrically valuable tokens. To address these problems, we present GHOST (Geometry-Hierarchical Online Streaming Token Eviction), a training-free KV cache management framework that exploits the model's own 3D geometry outputs to evict redundant tokens online. GHOST introduces three mutually reinforcing innovations: a hierarchical dual-level importance scoring scheme, a privilege mechanism that protects special tokens from eviction, and a cosine-similarity-guided layer-wise budget allocation. Experiments on various benchmarks show that GHOST preserves excellent reconstruction quality while cutting the KV cache by nearly half and delivering 1.75x faster inference compared to state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/lokiniuniu/GHOST.

</details>


### [152] [Paris 2.0: A Decentralized Diffusion Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26064)
*Ali Rouzbayani,Bidhan Roy,Marcos Villagra,Zhiying Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paris 2.0是首个通过去中心化计算预训练的视频生成模型，相比集中式训练模型在FVD指标上提升约2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Paris 1.0已证明图像生成可通过去中心化训练实现，但时序连贯的视频生成在去中心化训练下仍是一个开放问题，Paris 2.0旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: 基于Paris 1.0的去中心化扩散模型（DDM）框架，扩展至视频生成任务，采用低分辨率文本到视频训练方式。

Result: 在低分辨率文本到视频训练中，相比在相同数据和总计算预算下训练的集中式模型，Paris 2.0将FVD从561.04降至279.01（约2倍改进），同时提升了CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分。

Conclusion: Paris 2.0成功证明了视频生成也可以通过去中心化训练实现，且性能优于同等计算预算下的集中式训练模型。

Abstract: We present Paris 2.0, the first video generation model pre-trained through decentralized computation. Its training recipe builds upon Paris 1.0 (arXiv:2510.03434), the first ever open-weight Decentralized Diffusion Model (DDM), which showed that image generation can be trained without a monolithic GPU cluster. However, temporally coherent video generation had remained an open problem under decentralized training, and Paris 2.0 closes it.
  In low-resolution text-to-video training, against a monolithic model trained on the same data under a matched total compute budget, Paris 2.0 cuts Frechet Video Distance (FVD) from 561.04 to 279.01, a ~2.0x improvement, and lifts CLIP text-video similarity and aesthetic score.

</details>


### [153] [Reinforcing Few-step Generators via Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26108)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Ruoyu Wang,Chi Zhang,Jun Zhang,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RTDMD，一个两阶段框架，将分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导的强化学习统一起来，用于少步流生成器。第一阶段通过AC-DMD进行子区间分布匹配与一致性正则化，第二阶段联合优化分布匹配和奖励最大化项，在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上仅需4步推理即达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏虽能高效生成图像，但使模型与人类偏好对齐仍然困难。现有方法在少步生成中难以兼顾生成质量与奖励引导的对齐。

Method: 提出RTDMD两阶段框架：1) 第一阶段AC-DMD，通过子区间分布匹配和一致性正则化（fake score目标增强）来跟踪生成器分布；2) 第二阶段联合优化KL散度分解出的分布匹配项和奖励最大化项，奖励最大化项采用混合策略梯度（GRPO风格估计器用于随机中间过渡+直接反向传播用于确定性最终步），并引入SubGRPO降低方差。

Result: 在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上，仅需4步推理即在偏好、美观度和组合性指标上超越此前所有少步文本到图像生成方法，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: RTDMD通过统一分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导强化学习，有效解决了少步扩散生成与人类偏好对齐的难题，证明了将KL散度分解为两项并分别优化策略的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in few-step diffusion distillation have enabled efficient image generation, yet aligning these models with human preferences remains challenging. We propose Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that unifies distribution matching distillation with reward-guided reinforcement learning for few-step flow generators. We show that minimizing the KL divergence to a reward-tilted teacher distribution naturally decomposes into a distribution matching term and a reward maximization term. In the first stage, we introduce Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), which performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and augments the fake score objective with a consistency regularizer to help the fake score model track the shifting generator distribution under limited updates. In the second stage, we jointly optimize both terms: for the reward maximization term, we derive a hybrid policy gradient that combines a GRPO-style estimator for the stochastic intermediate transitions with direct reward backpropagation through the deterministic final step, and further introduce step-subset GRPO (SubGRPO) to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 demonstrate that RTDMD establishes new state-of-the-art results across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics with only 4 inference steps, outperforming previous few-step text-to-image generation methods. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD.

</details>


### [154] [ROVER: Routing Object-Centric Visual Evidence for Grounded Multi-Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27959)
*Guannan Lv,Ren Nie,Hongjian Dou,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROVER，一种轻量级可学习插件，通过对象中心差分注意力机制和步骤特定令牌三元组，在全局视觉证据路由中实现高效的多图像推理，在MM-GCoT和VideoEspresso基准上分别提升+4.8%和+8.6%的答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在视觉证据定位与推理中存在两难：基于RoI的方法削弱整体场景理解和对象间关系，且解码成本随RoI规模增加；自适应特征选择则需要细粒度监督或复杂启发式规则。需要一种既能保持全局场景理解又能高效路由视觉证据的轻量级解决方案。

Method: ROVER是一种轻量级可学习插件，核心设计包括：(1) 步骤特定令牌三元组，分别用于聚合推理上下文、通过对象中心差分注意力蒸馏图像内线索、路由和整合跨对象和跨图像的历史感知证据；(2) 对象中心差分注意力机制，将视觉信息压缩到视觉工作空间；(3) 集成到Qwen2.5-VL-7B中，采用SFT到GRPO的交错训练管道。

Result: 在MM-GCoT上答案准确率提升+4.8%，定位准确率提升+14.6%；在VideoEspresso上答案准确率提升+8.6%。VideoEspresso训练模型展现出强迁移性，在多个基准上平均超越基线模型+4.7%。

Conclusion: ROVER有效解决了现有方法在全局场景理解与局部视觉证据路由之间的矛盾，通过轻量级插件设计实现了高效的多图像推理，且具有良好的跨基准迁移能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have increasingly localized and interleaved visual evidence for deliberative reasoning. Grounding-based approaches typically focus on regions of interest (RoIs) by injecting cropped image patches or RoI-specific features into the reasoning context. However, such designs can weaken holistic scene understanding and inter-object relations, while incurring decoding costs that scale with the number and size of RoIs. Alternatively, adaptive visual feature selection often requires fine-grained supervision or complex heuristics. To address these limitations, we propose ROVER (Routing Object-centric Visual Evidence for grounded multi-image Reasoning), a lightweight, learnable plugin for efficient global visual evidence routing. Upon each object grounding prediction, ROVER injects a step-specific token triplet to synergistically: (i) aggregate the ongoing reasoning context, (ii) distill intra-image cues into a visual working space via object-centric differential attention, and (iii) route and integrate history-aware evidence across objects and images within this space for subsequent reasoning. We integrate ROVER into Qwen2.5-VL-7B and develop an interleaved SFT-to-GRPO training pipeline. Strictly adhering to the original datasets and evaluation protocols, our method achieves the best performance on MM-GCoT (+4.8% answer accuracy, +14.6% grounding accuracy) and VideoEspresso (+8.6% answer accuracy). The VideoEspresso-trained model demonstrates strong transferability, outperforming the base model by +4.7% on average across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [Diffusion Models, Denoiser Architecture and Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16415)
*Itamar Levine,Yair Weiss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验证明，扩散模型的创造力源于去噪器架构与目标分布之间的相互作用，而非模型本身的固有特性。不同去噪器架构（线性、多项式、瓶颈）会生成不同分布的样本，且UNET架构的微小变化会显著影响生成质量，表明去噪器的归纳偏差必须与目标分布高度对齐才能成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释扩散模型为何能够生成与训练数据不同的高真实感图像（即创造力），尽管理论上贝叶斯最优去噪器只会复制训练样本。

Method: 理论推导了三种去噪器架构（线性、多项式、瓶颈）下生成样本分布的显式形式；实验上通过修改UNET去噪器架构的微小变化，观察其对生成结果的影响。

Result: 不同去噪器架构会导致不同的创造力表现形式；UNET架构的微小变化可能产生高度不真实的样本；去噪器的归纳偏差必须与目标分布强对齐才能成功生成。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的成功取决于去噪器架构的归纳偏差与真实目标分布之间的强对齐程度。

Abstract: The creativity of diffusion models refers to their ability to generate highly realistic images that are different from their training data. Creativity is somewhat surprising since it is known that if the denoiser used in the diffusion model is the Bayes optimal denoiser for a given training set, then the model will simply copy the training samples. In this paper we present empirical and theoretical results that suggest that creativity in diffusion models is due to an interaction between the denoiser architecture and the target distribution. Theoretically, we give explicit forms for the distribution of generated samples as a function of the target distribution and the denoiser architecture for three different denoiser architectures (linear, polynomial, bottleneck). Empirically, we show that small changes in the popular UNET denoiser architecture leads to very different forms of creativity, and these small changes often yield samples that are highly nonrealistic. Taken together, our results show that diffusion models will only be successful if the inductive bias of the denoiser architecture is in strong alignment with the true target distribution.

</details>


### [156] [TWINGS: Thin Plate Splines Warp-aligned Initialization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22069)
*Hyeseong Kim,Geonhui Son,Deukhee Lee,Dosik Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWINGS是一种通过薄板样条(TPS)变形模型校准反投影点、为3D高斯泼溅提供几何精确初始化，从而在稀疏视图输入下实现高质量场景重建的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图输入下的新颖视图合成面临挑战，现有方法难以在有限视角下实现高质量场景重建，特别是点云稀疏性问题严重影响3DGS的初始化质量。

Method: 采用薄板样条(TPS)平滑非刚性变形模型，通过最小化弯曲能量从控制点对应关系估计全局一致的扭曲，将深度估计的反投影点与三角化的3D控制点对齐，生成校准后的反投影点，并从中采样以提供快速且几何精确的3DGS初始化。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的大量实验表明，TWINGS在稀疏视图场景下持续优于现有方法，能够生成细节丰富且精确的重建结果。

Conclusion: 通过直接解决点云稀疏性问题并利用TPS进行几何校准，TWINGS有效提升了3DGS在稀疏视图下的结构细节保持和色彩保真度，为稀疏视图三维重建提供了高效且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse-view inputs poses a significant challenge in 3D computer vision, particularly for achieving high-quality scene reconstructions with limited viewpoints. We introduce TWINGS, a framework that enhances 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by directly addressing point sparsity. We employ Thin Plate Splines (TPS), a smooth non-rigid deformation model that minimizes bending energy to estimate a globally coherent warp from control-point correspondences, to align backprojected points from estimated depth with triangulated 3D control points, yielding calibrated backprojected points. By sampling these calibrated points near the control points, TWINGS provides a fast and geometrically accurate initialization for 3DGS, ultimately improving structural detail preservation and color fidelity in reconstructed scenes. Extensive experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF360 demonstrate that TWINGS consistently outperforms existing methods, delivering detailed and accurate reconstructions under sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [157] [Nano World Models: A Minimalist Implementation of Future Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23993)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Kaiwen Geng,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nano World Models 是一个基于扩散强迫（diffusion forcing）的极简代码库，为未来视频预测提供统一接口，支持多种生成目标、模型规模、动作条件机制等，旨在为世界模型研究提供可复现、易扩展的实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界快速发展的交互式视频生成虽取得了显著进展，但更广泛的研究社区仍缺乏紧凑、可复现且易于扩展的实现来研究现代世界模型的设计选择，现有实现中各组件的设计选择往往相互纠缠，难以进行受控实验。

Method: 构建了基于扩散强迫（diffusion forcing）的极简代码库Nano World Models，提供统一的接口以支持生成目标、模型规模、动作条件机制、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长视距 rollout 程序，实现了各组件的模块化解耦。

Result: 通过在简单控制环境、游戏模拟和真实机器人数据上的实验，系统地考察了预测参数化、架构规模、动作注入、采样预算和领域复杂度对视频预测质量和自回归 rollout 行为的影响。

Conclusion: Nano World Models 提供了一个紧凑且可扩展的实验基础平台，通过开源代码、配置、评估脚本和预训练检查点，推动开放、可复现和科学化的世界模型研究。

Abstract: World models have become a central paradigm for learning predictive simulators that support generation, planning, and decision-making. Yet, despite rapid progress in industry-scale interactive video generation, the broader research community still lacks compact, reproducible, and easily extensible implementations for studying the design choices underlying modern world models. We introduce Nano World Models, a minimalist codebase for future video prediction centered around diffusion forcing. Nano World Models provides a unified interface for generative objectives, model scales, action-conditioning mechanisms, latent observation spaces, datasets, evaluation protocols, and long-horizon rollout procedures. This design enables controlled studies of world-modeling components that are often entangled across separate implementations. Through experiments across simple control environments, game simulation, and real-robot data, we examine how prediction parameterization, architecture scale, action injection, sampling budget, and domain complexity affect video prediction quality and autoregressive rollout behavior. By releasing code, configurations, evaluation scripts, and pretrained checkpoints, Nano World Models aims to provide a compact yet extensible experimental substrate for open, reproducible, and scientific world-model research.

</details>


### [158] [Structure over Pixels: Learning Variable-Length Visual Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27696)
*Piotr Wyrwiński,Kacper Dobek,Krzysztof Krawiec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STROP是一种离散视觉分词器架构，通过四阶段课程学习和冻结的DINOv3特征驱动的率-失真探针，学习为每张图像动态预测最优的视觉程序长度，并形成结构化场景表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应分词器要么需要事后搜索，要么在预训练码率中离散选择，无法学习与模型和场景耦合的连续每图像序列长度，且通常针对像素重建训练，强调纹理而非结构。

Method: 提出STROP架构，包含专用长度头，通过四阶段课程学习（由冻结的DINOv3特征提供的局部率-失真探针监督），单次前向传播即可估计活跃前缀长度。码本完全由高层潜在表示质量塑造，绕过像素级重建梯度。

Result: 程序长度随场景复杂度增长；在下游密集预测迁移任务和直接检查学习到的码本词汇中，均出现了组合式结构迹象。

Conclusion: STROP能够学习与场景复杂度耦合的连续序列长度，形成结构化表示，且无需像素级重建即可获得具有组合性的离散视觉码本。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenizers translate images into ordered sequences of codes, providing a natural representation for structural description of scenes. Yet existing adaptive tokenizers either require post-hoc search or select among a discrete set of pre-trained rates, rather than learning a continuous per-image sequence length coupled to the model and scene, and they typically train against pixel reconstruction, emphasizing texture rather than structure. We propose STROP, a discrete visual tokenizer architecture that forms structural scene representations and simultaneously learns how long an image's visual program should be. Using a four-phase curriculum supervised by local rate--distortion probes against frozen DINOv3 features, STROP optimizes a dedicated length head that estimates the active prefix length in a single forward pass. By bypassing pixel-level reconstruction gradients, the codebook is shaped entirely by the quality of higher-level latent representations. Program length grows with scene complexity, and signs of compositional structure emerge both in downstream dense-prediction transfer and in direct inspection of the learned code vocabulary.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Subcortical Shape Variations and Their Associations with Cognition Across the 8th Decade of Life. A Study in the Lothian Birth Cohort 1936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29703)
*Maria del C. Valdes-Hernandez,Wonjung Park,Joanna Moodie,Susana Muñoz Maniega,Janie Corley,Fraser N. Sneden,Mark E. Bastin,Joanna M. Wardlaw,Simon R. Cox,Jinah Park*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究利用Lothian Birth Cohort 1936纵向数据，分析社区老年人在80多岁期间皮层下脑结构的形态变化及其与认知衰老的关系。研究发现皮层下结构形态变化具有异质性，不同脑区表现出不同的萎缩模式，且认知变化主要与特定顶点位移相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规的脑容量测量可能无法全面反映与功能相关的脑衰老特征，而皮层下脑结构在认知中起重要作用，但其形态变化轨迹与认知衰老的关联尚未被充分记录。

Method: 利用Lothian Birth Cohort 1936的大样本纵向研究数据，采用ANCOVA和混合线性模型分析皮层下脑结构的形态变化及其与认知衰老的关联。

Result: 皮层下结构形态变化呈现异质性：海马和腹侧DC在不同半球表现出不同的变形模式，而丘脑和苍白球则呈现较均匀且近乎对称的体积收缩。一般认知变化主要与不同时间点之间的向内和向外顶点位移相关。

Conclusion: 皮层下脑结构的形态变化在80岁期间具有异质性，且这些变化与认知衰老存在关联，提示形态学指标可能比单纯体积测量更敏感地反映功能相关的脑衰老。

Abstract: The study of brain morphology changes in normal individuals may capture aspects of functionally-relevant brain aging not fully indicated by gross volumetry. Despite the important role of subcortical brain structures in cognition, the associations between their morphological trajectories and cognitive changes in aging have not been documented. We use neuroimaging, demographic, and cognitive data from a large longitudinal study of cognitive aging, the Lothian Birth Cohort 1936, to explore shape changes in subcortical brain structures of community-dwelling individuals across their 8th decade of life. We investigate the association of these changes with cognitive aging using ANCOVA and mixed linear model analyses. Subcortical shape changes were heterogeneous, with varied atrophy patterns across whole period. The hippocampus and the ventral DC experienced varied morphological deformations (from its baseline point) different in left and right hemispheres, while the thalami and globus pallidi shapes, for example, experienced a more uniform volume contraction, nearly symmetrical throughout different timelines. Changes in general cognition were mainly associated with inwards and outwards vertex displacements between the time-points.

</details>


### [160] [Inward rectifier potassium channels interact with calcium channels to promote robust and physiological bistability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14107)
*Anaëlle De Worm,Guillaume Drion,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过最小电导模型和双房室模型，发现背角投射神经元的中枢敏化依赖于CaL通道与Kir通道的组合，而非KM通道。CaL+Kir组合能产生更大的双稳窗口、更强的噪声鲁棒性，其机制在于Kir电流在阈电位附近存在负微分电导区。分岔分析表明CaL+Kir产生平台电位双稳，而CaL+KM产生谐振型兴奋性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明中枢敏化过程中背角投射神经元从紧张性发放转变为平台电位和持续后放电的内在机制，特别是电压门控L型钙通道与何种钾通道组合才能产生稳健且生理可实现的神经元双稳性。

Method: 采用最小电导模型（最小模型）进行理论分析，结合双房室模型（含真实离子通道组合）验证，运用分岔分析和数值模拟研究CaL与Kir或KM通道组合的双稳特性及兴奋性类型。

Result: CaL+Kir组合产生的双稳窗口显著大于CaL+KM组合，且对噪声和内在变异更鲁棒；Kir电流在阈电位附近具有负微分电导区，这是其扩大双稳窗口的关键；分岔分析显示CaL+Kir产生平台电位双稳，CaL+KM产生谐振型动力。

Conclusion: CaL+Kir通道对是中枢敏化中神经元双稳性和平台电位的候选内在机制，与KM通道相比，Kir通过其独特的电流形状特征更有效地支持生理双稳。

Abstract: Projection neurons in the dorsal horn relay nociceptive input to supraspinal centers. During central sensitization, a subset of them switches from tonic firing to plateau potentials with sustained afterdischarges, a change that requires intrinsic bistability between a resting and a spiking state. Voltage-gated L-type calcium (CaL) channels can produce bistability, but reach physiological resting states only when paired with voltage-gated potassium channels, most of which simultaneously shrink the bistability window. How robust, physiological bistability arises has therefore remained unclear. Using a minimal conductance-based model, we show that inward rectifier potassium (Kir) channels enlarge the bistability window when combined with CaL channels, while M-type potassium (KM) channels slightly reduce it. Within the parameter region where bistability is both robust and physiological, both channel types can sustain bistability, but the CaL+Kir combination produces a substantially larger window and is more robust to noise and intrinsic variability. This window-enlarging effect traces to a shape feature of the outward Kir steady-state current: like the CaL current, it has a region of negative differential conductance around the spike threshold, a feature absent from KM and from most other voltage-gated potassium currents. Bifurcation analysis further shows that the two pairs support qualitatively distinct excitability: plateau-generating bistability for CaL+Kir and resonator-like dynamics for CaL+KM. These conclusions hold in a two-compartment model of deep projection neurons with realistic ion channel complements, and identify the CaL+Kir pair as a candidate intrinsic mechanism for central sensitization.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Neural-Behavioral Representation of Natural Whole-body Movement in Monkeys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29355)
*Jieshi He,Puzhe Li,Yanan Sui,Mu-ming Poo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自由移动灵长类动物的神经-行为记录与建模框架，结合大规模硬膜外皮层信号和多视角动作捕捉，利用自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑行为先验，实现了无需物理约束的全身运动解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究受限于动作多样性和大规模全身运动神经表征的可及性，仅关注受限任务和有限肢体运动。作者旨在突破这些限制，实现自然全身行为的神经解码。

Method: 构建定制数据采集平台，同步记录自由移动猕猴的分布式感觉-运动区硬膜外皮层信号和多视角动作捕捉数据；使用自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑行为先验，并以神经信号为条件进行解码。

Result: 模型能够在无需显式物理约束的条件下，解码出准确且逼真的全身运动，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用大规模颅内神经活动解码灵长类自然全身运动提供了概念验证性新途径。

Abstract: Understanding how cortical activity represents natural whole-body behaviors in primates remains challenging. Limited by the diversity of movements and inaccessibility of large-scale neural representation of whole-body kinematics, previous motor decoding studies focused on constrained tasks and limited limb movements. Here, we present a neural-behavioral recording and modeling framework for freely moving monkeys, combining large-scale epidural cortical signals from distributed sensory- and motor-related areas with synchronized multi-view motion capture through a custom-made data collection platform. We reconstructed whole-body monkey kinematics and learned a compact behavior prior using an autoregressive encoder-decoder model. Conditioned on neural signals, the model decoded accurate and realistic whole-body movement without explicit physical constraints. Our results provide a novel proof-of-concept approach for decoding natural whole-body movements in primates using large-scale intracranial neural activity.

</details>


### [162] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法虽然修改不同的MLP权重，但都依赖于一个共同的功能子空间：通过抑制而非覆盖原有知识，导致后层过度注意力的消除。这一机制对编辑成功至关重要，也解释了为何编辑无法传播到相关事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME和MEMIT）主要通过输出行为评估效果，但其内部工作机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究不同事实编辑是否依赖共同机制，以及该机制如何影响编辑成功率和知识传播能力。

Method: 在编辑后的MLP权重上训练一个紧凑的二元掩码（binary mask），该掩码能够反转编辑效果。通过分析掩码的作用模式，识别出编辑共用的关键权重子集。同时，通过注入掩码观察编辑成功率的变化，验证该机制的必要性。

Result: 该掩码在训练集上反转了80%的编辑，测试集上超过70%，证实不同编辑共享共同的功能结构。掩码通过消除后层过度注意力来反转编辑。注入掩码后，编辑成功率从98%骤降至38%，表明该机制对编辑成功必不可少。

Conclusion: 知识编辑的本质是抑制而非覆盖原有知识，这解释了为何ROME和MEMIT无法传播到相关事实。识别的共同功能子空间可用于检测和防御不良编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [163] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过TradeArena测试平台研究LLM智能体在金融决策中的行为对齐与表征动态，发现规划嵌入漂移、有效秩收缩等预失效信号，并验证了结构化风险反馈作为外部对齐信号的有效性及局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动态，特别是在市场压力下的理性、持仓和干预如何演变，以识别对齐失败或失效前的可测量信号。

Method: 使用TradeArena可审计交易智能体测试平台（含风险报告、执行模拟、记忆和可回放轨迹），通过80个滚动失败锚点覆盖8条LLM轨迹，采用多种嵌入方法（哈希、LSA、Transformer、白盒隐藏状态探针）进行流形诊断，并设计消融实验（无思维链、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计报告）进行压力测试。

Result: 发现规划嵌入漂移、有效秩收缩等预失效信号；结构化风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号但非通用性能增强器；51只股票日内实验揭示相关性盲点——LLM理性常为风险层反复截断的集中持仓提供理由。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈与表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时对齐、漂移或失效，本研究支持研究声称而非盈利声称。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [164] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出微分电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability）这一头部级度量，用于比较RL和SFT在微调大语言模型时对内部计算电路的破坏程度。研究发现：SFT适应目标任务更快，但导致更大的电路破坏和先前能力遗忘；RL保留了更多基础电路，但任务适应较慢。电路保留程度可能是RL抗灾难性遗忘更强的机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已观察到RL比SFT更能保留先验能力（行为层面），但缺乏对其背后机制（即内部计算电路层面）的理解。本文旨在从 mechanistic 层面探究RL抗遗忘优势的根源，验证电路保留程度是否与抗遗忘能力相关。

Method: 提出微分电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability），一种基于注意力头部（head-level）的度量，衡量微调对特定电路组件的退化程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上，针对科学问答任务，分别使用RL和SFT进行微调，比较两种方法对基础电路的影响差异。

Result: SFT导致更严重的电路破坏和更大的先验能力遗忘，但目标任务适应更快；RL保留了更多基础电路，抗遗忘更强，但任务适应速度较慢。存在清晰的 mechanistic 权衡：电路保留程度与任务适应速度呈负相关。

Conclusion: 电路保留（circuit preservation）可能解释了RL相比SFT更能抵抗灾难性遗忘的原因。微分电路脆弱性为理解微调过程中模型内部机制的变化提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [165] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种轨迹感知的LLM推理智能体，将分子先导优化中的工具选择建模为序列决策问题，在ADMET优化任务上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子先导优化方法多为单步优化，未能考虑序列设计决策的长期影响，需要一种能够进行前瞻性推理的轨迹感知方法。

Method: 提出TRACE（Trajectory-Aware, LLM-Reasoning Agent for Molecular Lead Optimization），将工具选择形式化为动作轨迹上的序列决策问题，使智能体能够在结构约束下进行前瞻性优化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务上，TRACE实现了更高的优化成功率、更大的性质改进和更高的有效性，同时保持与基线模型相当的分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的序列决策方法能更有效地解决分子先导优化问题，LLM推理智能体在此类多步优化任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [166] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究四人非完美信息纸牌游戏Big 2，开发自对弈强化学习框架，比较策略梯度和值函数近似方法。实验表明PPO优于蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q学习，适度熵正则化可防止策略过于确定性，当前策略自对弈比检查点自对弈或固定对手训练提供更强的有限预算课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非完美信息多人博弈中智能体在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手条件下的表现，将Big 2作为受控实验环境研究深度强化学习方法。

Method: 开发自对弈RL框架，在统一环境下控制输入表示、训练预算和评估协议，对比PPO、蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning等算法，并研究熵正则化和不同自对弈策略的影响。

Result: PPO在随机、贪心和启发式对手上均优于其他方法；适度熵正则化提升PPO性能；当前策略自对弈比检查点自对弈和固定对手训练效果更好。

Conclusion: Big 2是研究深度强化学习在非完美信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下的有效受控实验平台。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [167] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的物理探索中，其潜在空间会自发形成与物理几何结构对齐的空间语义表征，且预测性能与语义对齐共同演化，验证了物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有语言监督的条件下，世界模型从物理探索中能学到什么，以及其潜在表征的组织原则。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，分析潜在空间的空间语义结构；通过时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系；采用KL正则化双敲除实验验证共享驱动假设。

Result: 方向准确率0.677±0.029（随机编码器0.547），位置RSA 0.192±0.047（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐显著共变（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；标准KL正则化（beta=0.1）使两者同时崩溃，降低beta至0.001则同时恢复。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则，这一发现对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体有直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [168] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COM策略，通过几何约束保留时间序列token的连续性和有序性，显著提升基于token的时间序列大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型忽略了时间序列token固有的连续性和有序性，严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，在初始化和训练阶段集成几何约束，以保留时间序列token的连续性和有序性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上，COM一致地提升了基于token的TS-LLM性能，取得了有竞争力的结果和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列token嵌入中的连续性和有序性对于基于token的TS-LLM的有效性至关重要，COM策略能有效提升模型性能。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [169] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow 是一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的新方法，通过引入Koopman启发的动力学专家和置信度感知的赢家通吃（WTA）目标，解决标准流匹配在处理多模态、多尺度时间序列数据时的谱收缩和模态覆盖不足问题，在多项基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列信号常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（包括振荡和高频变化）。标准流匹配使用单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器，在异构时间分布中不同模态可能经过相近流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度，导致估计器学习到过度平滑的局部传输场近似，引发谱失真和模态覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow，包含三个关键组件：(1) Koopman启发的动力学专家——每个专家在潜在空间中学习残差校正，将局部非线性时间演化近似为线性转移；(2) 置信度感知的赢家通吃（WTA）目标——仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，屏蔽其他专家的梯度，促进模态特异性专业化；(3) 采样时，选中的专家向全局传输场添加残差动力学校正，在保持FM稳定性的同时恢复细粒度高频时间结构。

Result: 在多项基准上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准FM的谱收缩问题，实现SOTA性能：Context-FID提升15.6%，判别性分数（Discriminative Score）改善38.6%，且在低数据场景下保持鲁棒性，对预测和插补任务同样有效。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过引入多个Koopman动力学专家和WTA训练策略，成功解决了标准流匹配在多模态时间序列生成中的模态混合和频率失真问题，在不牺牲稳定性的前提下恢复了细粒度时域结构，验证了该方法在时间序列生成、预测和插补等任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [170] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平衡多模态标签重塑（BMLR）方法，从标签侧解决多模态学习中因各模态到标签空间映射难度不同导致的学习速度不平衡问题，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡映射难度，从而提升多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中存在模态不平衡现象，某些模态收敛更快并主导优化过程，而其他模态训练不足。现有方法主要通过强化弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解，但往往牺牲强模态的优化能力，且未从模态层面分析不平衡产生的根本原因。

Method: 提出BMLR方法，从标签侧设计促进多模态平衡。通过重塑跨模态标签空间，使各模态到标签空间的映射难度均等化，从而促进模态交互并为每个模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR方法能持续提升多模态性能，并且与多种模型设计具有良好的兼容性。

Conclusion: 从标签侧入手解决模态学习速度差异是一种有效的新思路，BMLR通过均衡映射难度实现了模态间的更好平衡，且不影响强模态的优化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [171] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏差和噪声的三元统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说（PRH），提出柏拉图对齐源于物体与属性之间的普遍关系（信号），可通过稀疏自编码器提取的线性表征来解释；模型间的偏差可通过中心化和归一化部分缓解；有限样本训练导致的噪声与数据稀缺相关（词频与对齐度正相关）。最终提出一个改进线性表征假说的统计模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同AI模型表征之间为何会出现柏拉图式的对齐现象（PRH），并试图从信号、偏差和噪声三个维度系统性地解释其形成机制。

Method: 1) 信号层面：利用稀疏自编码器提取线性物体-属性特征，验证线性表征假说（LRH）对PRH的解释力；2) 偏差层面：通过中心化和归一化操作缓解不同架构和训练过程带来的隐式偏差；3) 噪声层面：分析词频与表征对齐度的相关性，证明数据稀缺驱动表征噪声。最终整合三方面提出统计模型。

Result: 稀疏表征比稠密表征具有更强的跨模态对齐；中心化和归一化能持续改善跨模型对齐；词频与对齐度之间存在强且一致的正相关关系。

Conclusion: 提出了一个包含信号、偏差和噪声的统计框架，改进了线性表征假说，并为理解现代AI架构中涌现的表征对齐现象提供了更全面的解释模型。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [172] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将经典配对二项样本量计算方法适配至量化基准测试的保守最小可检测效应（MDE）界，并通过对四种模型和四个基准的预注册试点审计及MMLU提示模板对比研究，验证了该界在区分量化噪声与二项抽样噪声、提示模板噪声中的实用性，最终提供了五行的预注册模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试中，研究者常报告NF4与FP16间的性能差异，但缺乏系统方法判断观测到的差异是真实量化效应还是抽样噪声。现有工作未将基准不可靠性与二项抽样噪声直接关联，也没有在量化审计中控制提示模板方差。本文旨在将“我的量化声称有多可靠？”这一问题转化为基准设计者可在实验前承诺的单行预算。

Method: 将Miettinen（1968）的经典配对二项样本量计算方法适配至量化基准测试场景，推导出保守MDE界 δ^{*} ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，其中m为配对项数，ρ_d为FP16-NF4不一致率。在四种模型和四个基准上（k=5次分割，n=100）进行试点审计，并并行开展MMLU提示模板变化研究。最后提供五行预注册模板。

Result: 假设ρ_d=0.10（未测量的规划值），所有观测到的NF4-FP16差值均低于隐含MDE；多数跨分割标准差落在±1.5个百分点内于二项参考值√(p(1-p)/n)，表明n=100子样本上报告的大部分“基准不可靠性”方差实为二项抽样噪声。OPT-WinoGrande单元格（|Δ|=3.2 pp）在ρ_d=0.10时低于MDE，但在ρ_d=0.05时高于MDE，凸显了规划权衡。MMLU上提示模板变化范围（2-10 pp）达到或超过最大观测量化差值（3.2 pp）。

Conclusion: 所提MDE界能将量化声称的可靠性问题转化为可事先承诺的预算，为基准设计者提供了实用工具。量化审计必须先固定提示模板，否则模板方差会被吸收进噪声基底。结合五行预注册模板，该方法有助于提升量化基准测试的可重复性和统计严谨性。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [173] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文蒸馏重构为潜记忆管理问题，通过独立LoRA适配器构建模块化记忆库，引入Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜记忆，并结合缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法显著优于基线，Self-Gating能有效去激活不必要记忆以增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了在非oracle场景下多个蒸馏潜记忆应如何存储、检索及安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立的LoRA适配器，形成模块化记忆库；2) 查询时检索候选记忆并路由至最合适适配器；3) 使用Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜记忆；4) 引入缓存共享降低推理管理开销。

Result: 本方法在检索场景下显著优于基线，Self-Gating通过去激活不必要的潜记忆提升了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏视为潜记忆管理问题，通过模块化LoRA记忆库与Self-Gating机制，实现了更安全、可控的记忆激活，并提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [174] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）研究LoRA微调引起的表示几何变化，发现LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间存在较弱的几何对齐，且LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表示结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为广泛使用的大模型微调方法，但其内部表示变化尚未被充分理解，因此需要深入探究LoRA微调对模型内部表示几何结构的影响。

Method: 提出delta激活框架隔离适配器对残差流的贡献，使用Gemma-2-9B在LoRA秩4、8、16、32下训练适配器专用SAE，通过余弦相似度、主角度分析和中心核对齐（CKA）评估表示对齐程度。

Result: LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间几何对齐较弱；适配器专用SAE重建delta激活的效果优于预训练SAE；特征密度随秩和深度增加而增加，而几何发散在不同秩下保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调能够诱导出预训练可解释性字典无法完全捕获的特征结构，这对精细调优模型的机制可解释性、适配分析及安全审计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [175] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的固有几何结构控制扩散模型，通过条件期望算子的奇异函数实现无需重训练或反向传播的高保真控制，在CIFAR-10上条件精度提升37个百分点且加速4倍，并揭示了有效引导的最佳时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中噪声逐步破坏数据，仅少量特征对控制保持信息量，现有方法需要重训练或反向传播，效率低下且不稳定。

Method: 将信息特征刻画为条件期望算子的奇异函数，通过自监督学习恢复该基函数，然后将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码）投影到采样轨迹上。

Result: CIFAR-10上条件精度比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍；同一表示支持标签、CLIP和空间（掩码）引导，无需辅助模型。

Conclusion: 揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，确定了有效引导的最优时间窗口。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [176] [Sequential Physics-Constrained Neural Operator Forward Modeling for the $\textit{Norne}$ Reservoir System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28909)
*Clement Etienam,Juntao Yang,Oleg Ovcharenko,Nick Luiken,Tsubasa Onishi,Nefeli Moridis,Issam Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于傅里叶神经算子(FNO/PINO)的三相黑油油藏序贯替代模型理论框架，并在Norne基准油藏(113,344网格, 3298天)上验证。理论涵盖四个问题：积Sobolev空间适定性、Wasserstein-2协变量偏移界、物理约束谱稳定性(滚动误差一致有界)、K步TBPTT梯度分析。实验表明自动回归PINO在$R^2>0.99$(油)/$>0.90$(气)/$\approx0.80$(压力)/单调上升(水)，单GPU千成员集合1分钟内完成，相比OPM有限体积模拟器加速约$10^4\times$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决油藏数值模拟中全阶有限体积模拟计算成本过高的问题，建立具有严格理论保证的神经算子替代模型，克服时序滚动推理中误差累积和协变量偏移等核心挑战。

Method: 采用FNO/PINO神经算子架构，在乘积Sobolev空间中建立隐式时间步映射的适定性理论；提出Wasserstein-2距离上界的协变量偏移分析；利用物理约束谱稳定性分析证明PINO训练可降低雅可比谱半径并保证滚动误差一致有界；导出K步截断BPTT的几何偏置衰减和最优窗口，证明Adam收敛率。

Result: PINO替代模型在3298天预测中：油相$R^2>0.99$，气相$R^2>0.90$，压力$R^2\approx0.80$，水相$R^2$单调提升。8块B200 GPU训练<1小时，单B200 GPU推理千成员集合<1分钟，相比OPM有限体积模拟器加速约$10^4$倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架与数值方案在油藏替代建模中实现了理论严谨性与计算效率的统一，为大规模地下流体模拟的深度学习替代方法提供了可证明的可靠性保障。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive mathematical and computational framework for sequential surrogate modeling of three-phase black-oil reservoir dynamics using neural operators, with particular emphasis on Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) and their physics-informed variant (PINO). The application focus is the Norne benchmark reservoir, defined on a heterogeneous $46\times112\times22$ grid ($N=113,344$ cells), with a production history spanning $T=30$ timesteps covering 3298 days. Our theoretical contributions are organized around four interlocking problems: (1) functional-analytic formulation in a product-Sobolev-space setting, including well-posedness of the implicit timestep map and sharp local Lipschitz estimates; (2) covariate shift quantification, proving that the Wasserstein-2 distance grows as $W_2 \leq \varepsilon(L^n-1)/(L-1)$, with exponential population-risk discrepancy for $L>1$; (3) physics-constrained spectral stability, showing PINO training with $λ_R \geq λ^*_R$ reduces the learned Jacobian spectral radius to $ρ_F + Cλ_R^{-1/2}$, yielding uniform-in-time rollout error $|δ_n| \leq \varepsilon/(1-ρ)$; and (4) $K$-step TBPTT gradient analysis, deriving geometric bias decay $O(ρ^K)$, optimal window $K^ = O(\log(T/σ^2))$, and Adam convergence $O(1/\sqrt{t}) + O(ρ^{K^*})$. Empirical validation confirms all theoretical predictions: autoregressive PINO surrogates sustain $R^2>0.99$ (oil), $R^2>0.90$ (gas), $R^2\approx 0.80$ (pressure), and monotonically improving $R^2$ (water) across the full 3298-day horizon, trained on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs in under one hour. A 1000-member ensemble runs in under one minute on a single B200 GPU, giving a ${\sim}10^4\times$ wall-clock speedup over the OPM finite-volume simulator.

</details>


### [177] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成奖励塑形（reward shaping）视为调试（debugging）而非一次性生成（one-shot generation）。通过PPO训练和MiniGrid/MuJoCo评估，识别出奖励泛滥（reward flooding）和语义/API误解两种主要失败模式，并设计了基于诊断的迭代精炼方法。在DoorKey-8x8上将成功率从2.3%提升至97.6%，在KeyCorridor上从31.2%提升至86.7%。控制实验表明，改进主要来自故障分类提示（taxonomy prompt），而非重试或额外训练。但该方法在连续控制的稠密奖励任务中表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对稀疏、结构化的强化学习任务，LLM生成的奖励塑形函数往往存在系统性失败模式，一次性生成质量不可靠。作者希望将问题从“生成正确奖励函数”重新定义为“识别并修复故障”，从而更高效地利用LLM。

Method: 1) 系统审计LLM生成奖励函数的两类失败模式（奖励泛滥、语义/API误解）及弱塑形情形。2) 提出诊断驱动的迭代精炼（diagnostic-driven iterative refinement），利用训练诊断和故障分类指导针对性修改。3) 通过多种对照实验（指标重提示、静态词汇表控制、预算匹配、Best-of-3、组件移除、敏感性分析）分离精炼效果来源。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%（高方差）。静态词汇表控制恢复大部分差距（87.6%；70.7%），说明故障分类提示是主要机制。连续控制（MuJoCo）中诊断驱动方法效果有限，成功率诊断在稠密奖励场景可能失效。

Conclusion: LLM生成奖励塑形应被框定为调试问题而非生成问题。该方法适用于PPO训练下的稀疏结构化任务，且需要可靠的奖励接口。故障分类提示是关键机制，动态标签仅提供部分增量证据。方法在连续控制边界上表现有限，需避免奖励泛滥和误报机制。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [178] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CosmicFish-HRM是一个紧凑型语言模型，通过分层推理模块(HRM)实现自适应推理深度，根据输入复杂度动态调整计算量，在小型模型上展示了非均匀推理行为，为替代单纯依靠参数规模提升推理能力提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在紧凑型语言模型中通过自适应推理深度而非单纯增加参数量来提升推理能力，以解决大规模模型参数多、推理成本高的问题。

Method: 提出CosmicFish-HRM模型，核心是分层推理模块(HRM)，通过高低级推理循环动态分配计算资源，结合Grouped Query Attention、RoPE和SwiGLU等现代Transformer组件，实现根据输入复杂度自适应停止推理。

Result: 模型学会了非均匀推理行为，能在不同任务和输入上分配不同数量的推理步骤；但小规模下推理基础设施带来额外开销，随着模型规模增大该开销相对减小。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能是一种有前景的替代方案，可以在不依赖单纯扩大参数规模的情况下提升语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [179] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了log-alignment ratio (LAR)，一种衡量参数-激活对齐程度的度量，并将其重新表述为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠。实验表明，在grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR能够有效追踪从记忆到泛化的转变、预测有效维度以及监测泛化差距，且计算成本极低，无需验证数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在训练过程中低成本地监测模型从记忆到泛化的转变，尤其是缺乏无需验证数据、可在前向传播中轻松计算的度量标准。LAR的提出旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将LAR重新定义为权重谱p（矩阵归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱q（输入在奇异方向上的归一化平方投影）之间的重叠。通过在grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练两个场景中分析LAR的动态变化来验证其有效性。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR可预测学习函数的有效维度k ≈ n^{2(1-LAR)}；在3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR与无过拟合基线的偏差可追踪泛化差距，其下降速率在过拟合临近时增加。

Conclusion: LAR是一个计算高效、无需验证数据的有效度量，能够可靠地监测训练中的泛化动态，具有在实际大规模训练中应用的前景。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [180] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用多种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）分析InceptionTime架构在EEG-based抑郁症检测中的决策依据，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其右半球）存在部分一致的归因模式，但方法间差异显著，提示事后可解释性在精神疾病应用中兼具价值与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在EEG-based抑郁症分类中性能不断提升，但其高容量模型的决策过程难以解释，阻碍了临床可信应用。因此需要系统评估不同事后可解释性方法在揭示模型决策依据方面的有效性和一致性。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行EEG-based MDD检测，在受试者级分层5折交叉验证框架下，应用五种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）进行全局归因聚合分析，并比较不同方法间的归因分布一致性。

Result: 不同可解释性方法揭示了部分收敛的归因模式，重点集中在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其右半球）。梯度法与扰动法之间一致性较高，而DeepSHAP的归因分布与其他方法差异显著。方法间变异性突显了方法学假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 不同事后可解释性方法捕捉到EEG深度学习模型中部分重叠的相关性结构，归因模式与既往EEG抑郁症研究大体一致，但分析应视为探索性而非神经生理生物标志物或临床应用的确定性证据。研究强调了事后可解释性在解释黑箱EEG分类器时的有用性与局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级自监督学习框架BrainSimSiam，利用正样本对从fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且通用的特征表示，在数据有限的神经影像应用中优于全监督基线并接近大规模模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据获取成本高、样本量小、标签质量不一，且数据维度极高，导致模型过拟合风险大；现有fMRI基础模型预训练和微调的计算资源需求过高。

Method: 采用轻量级自监督框架，基于正样本对（positive-only data pairs）进行对比学习，无需负样本，学习鲁棒且可泛化的特征表示。

Result: 在多个下游分类和回归任务中，BrainSimSiam学习到的表示优于全监督基线，并接近大规模模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam为数据有限的神经影像应用提供了一种高效、可扩展的自监督学习方案，验证了轻量级自监督方法在fMRI表示学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [182] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出形式不变性审计协议FormInv，发现改写质量缺陷会显著改变模型排名（如GPT-4o从第2跌至第4），且在标准基准上不可见。揭示语义一致性率（SCR）与准确率之间存在高达32个百分点的差距，并证明存在无免费基准推论：改写族的选择隐式决定了模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试依赖改写后的语义等价性来评估模型，但改写质量未经验证，导致排名结果不可靠；同时标准准确率指标无法捕捉模型在不同语义等价表达下的行为不一致性。

Method: 对MathCheck（ICLR 2025）进行改写质量审计，检测到3.1%的语义不正确改写；提出跨模型一致性协议（≥3/4模型）自动发现错误；提出FormInv协议，包含SCR和每定理Cochran's Q作为主要不变性度量，在Lean4验证定理上评估9个模型（366-811项）。

Result: 移除4个语义错误改写后GPT-4o从第2降至第4；Claude Haiku 4.5准确率86%但SCR仅50%（一半定理在等价重述下答案不同）；9模型准确率跨度仅86-96%但SCR跨度达50-82%（32点差距）；证明无免费基准推论：改写族权重可实现任何目标排名。

Conclusion: 标准基准无法捕捉语义一致性缺陷，形式不变性审计（FormInv）是评估模型鲁棒性的必要补充；基准设计者应意识到改写族选择隐式决定了排名结果。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [183] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRe——一种免训练、推理时可即插即用的方法，通过动态地仅评估一小部分高冲突/高不确定性的交互（而非固定稀疏化），在最大独立集问题（MIS）上实现超过3倍的可扩展性提升、约8倍加速和约12倍峰值内存降低；在旅行商问题（TSP）上实现约15倍加速和44倍内存降低，且解质量不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的神经求解器在推理时需要反复评估密集的边/因子交互，导致计算速度慢且受内存瓶颈限制，难以扩展到大规模问题。

Method: 受多体物理计算方法启发，LoRe在每步迭代中动态地仅评估固定比例的交互，将计算资源路由到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，而非使用静态kNN或固定掩码。

Result: 在MIS上：可扩展性提升超过3倍（超越基线OOM限制），约8倍加速，约12倍峰值内存降低，解质量保持；在TSP（n=1000）上：约15倍加速，44倍内存降低，路径质量有竞争力。此外还具有零样本拓扑鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRe作为一种训练无关、推理时即插即用的交互预算控制方法，能有效提升扩散神经求解器在大规模组合优化问题上的可扩展性和效率，且不牺牲解质量。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [184] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一种因果智能方法，通过学习上下文特定的因果图和结构因果模型，结合约束感知的多目标优化，实现从源状态到目标状态的可控系统转变，并能识别关键因果驱动因素和最优干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型缺乏机制性洞察力，也没有用于决策的框架，无法通过有针对性的干预将系统从一个状态驱动到另一个状态。

Method: COAST框架包含特征选择、因果发现、因果建模、干预识别与评估等模块化组件；学习上下文特定因果图和结构因果模型；将观测到的分布偏移归因于机制级因果驱动因素；采用约束感知的多目标优化，平衡过渡效能、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性。

Result: 在合成基准和真实生物数据集上，COAST成功恢复了关键因果驱动因素，识别出稳健的单目标和多目标干预策略，实现了期望的状态转变。

Conclusion: COAST为设计约束下的系统状态转变提供了透明、可解释的机制性决策框架，可指导实验验证。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [185] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型熵（CME），利用独立验证器模型对生成器响应的平均对数似然作为无标签奖励信号，用于强化学习后训练大语言模型，在开放式指令遵循任务中超越自参考信号方法，无需真实答案或人工偏好标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练依赖真实可验证奖励（限制领域）或人工偏好标签（昂贵且易受奖励破解）。无标签方法使用自参考信号（如多数投票、token熵）但会强化模型自身错误。需要一种无需标签、不可被生成器利用的奖励信号。

Method: 跨模型熵（CME）：计算生成器输出在独立验证器模型下的平均对数似然作为奖励信号。验证器与生成器无关，因此无法通过自一致性作弊。将CME集成到GRPO框架中，无需改动训练循环其他部分。
