To address this mismatch, we introduce \textbf{GSB-MDPO} (\emph{Generalized Schrödinger Bridge Mirror Descent Policy Optimization}), which formulates on-policy generative policy optimization as a Generalized Schrödinger Bridge problem over state-conditioned generation paths and instantiates the resulting path-measure update through mirror descent policy optimization. The key insight is that the GSB path-space KL plays the role of the proximal term in MDPO while upper-bounding the terminal action KL, enabling direct control of the executed action distribution without explicit terminal action likelihood evaluation. Experiments on 14 continuous-control tasks across Playground and Gym-MuJoCo demonstrate the empirical effectiveness of GSB-MDPO and support path-space regularization as a principled proximal update for multi-step generative policies.

</details>


### [335] [Learning Safely Without Knowing the World:COMPASS-Hedge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22348)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPASS-Hedge算法，首次在完全信息在线学习场景下同时实现了对抗环境下的极小化最优遗憾、随机环境下的实例最优（间隙相关）遗憾，以及相对于固定基准策略的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$遗憾，且无需任何先验知识，打破了在线学习中的三重困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习算法常面临对抗性环境、随机性环境与基准安全性三者之间的权衡困境，现有方法最多能兼顾其中两者，无法在不牺牲最优速率或依赖问题特定参数的条件下统一三种保证。

Method: 提出COMPASS-Hedge算法，核心创新包括：1）自适应伪遗憾缩放（adaptive pseudo-regret scaling）；2）基于阶段的激进策略（phase-based aggression）；3）比较器感知的混合策略（comparator-aware mixing strategy）。三者协同实现无需先验参数的统一最优保证。

Result: 在完全信息设定下，COMPASS-Hedge首次实现了"三世界最优"（best-of-three-world）保证：对抗环境达到极小化最优遗憾；随机环境达到实例最优间隙相关遗憾；相对于固定基准策略达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$的遗憾，所有结果均仅差对数因子。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基准安全性（baseline safety）不必以牺牲最坏情况鲁棒性或随机环境效率为代价，为在线学习中的多目标统一提供了首个完全信息解决方案。

Abstract: Online learning algorithms often face a fundamental trilemma: balancing regret guarantees between adversarial and stochastic settings and providing baseline safety against a fixed comparator. While existing methods excel in one or two of these regimes, they typically fail to unify all three without sacrificing optimal rates or requiring oracle access to problem-dependent parameters. In this work, we bridge this gap by introducing COMPASS-Hedge. To the best of our knowledge, our algorithm is the first full-information anytime method to simultaneously achieve, up to logarithmic factors: i) minimax-optimal regret in adversarial environments; ii) instance-optimal, gap-dependent regret in stochastic environments; and iii) $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$ regret relative to a designated baseline policy. Crucially, COMPASS-Hedge is parameter-free and requires no prior knowledge of the environment's nature or the magnitude of the stochastic suboptimality gaps. Our approach hinges on a novel integration of adaptive pseudo-regret scaling and phase-based aggression, coupled with a comparator-aware mixing strategy. To the best of our knowledge, this provides the first "best-of-three-world" guarantee in the full-information setting, establishing that baseline safety does not have to come at the cost of worst-case robustness or stochastic efficiency.

</details>


### [336] [Accelerating trajectory optimization with Sobolev-trained diffusion policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19011)
*Théotime Le Hellard,Franki Nguimatsia Tiofack,Quentin Le Lidec,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散策略结合轨迹优化求解器的反馈增益信息进行Sobolev学习，通过一阶损失函数有效避免了复合误差，使策略能够从极少轨迹中学习，将求解时间降低2-20倍，并减少推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹优化求解器对每个新问题独立求解，收敛速度和解质量高度依赖初始猜测。传统热启动方法中，基于学习的策略在 rollout 时因局部最优演示存在非最优偏差，导致复合误差累积，尤其在长时域任务中表现明显。

Method: 利用梯度型轨迹优化求解器提供的轨迹和反馈增益信息，推导了基于Sobolev学习的一阶损失函数，训练扩散策略同时匹配轨迹位置和导数信息（即反馈增益），使策略学习不仅拟合最优路径，还拟合最优路径邻域的局部几何结构。

Result: 实验表明，该方法有效避免了复合误差，能从极少量轨迹中学习，将求解时间降低2倍到20倍；同时，引入一阶信息允许使用更少的扩散步数进行预测，降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: 利用反馈增益进行Sobolev学习的扩散策略能够有效热启动轨迹优化求解器，缓解复合误差问题，实现高效、低延迟的初始猜测生成。

Abstract: Trajectory Optimization (TO) solvers exploit known system dynamics to compute locally optimal trajectories through iterative improvements. A downside is that each new problem instance is solved independently; therefore, convergence speed and quality of the solution found depend on the initial trajectory proposed. To improve efficiency, a natural approach is to warm-start TO with initial guesses produced by a learned policy trained on trajectories previously generated by the solver. Diffusion-based policies have recently emerged as expressive imitation learning models, making them promising candidates for this role. Yet, a counterintuitive challenge comes from the local optimality of TO demonstrations: when a policy is rolled out, small non-optimal deviations may push it into situations not represented in the training data, triggering compounding errors over long horizons. In this work, we focus on learning-based warm-starting for gradient-based TO solvers that also provide feedback gains. Exploiting this specificity, we derive a first-order loss for Sobolev learning of diffusion-based policies using both trajectories and feedback gains. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that the resulting policy avoids compounding errors, and so can learn from very few trajectories to provide initial guesses reducing solving time by $2\times$ to $20 \times$. Incorporating first-order information enables predictions with fewer diffusion steps, reducing inference latency.

</details>


### [337] [Negative Ontology of True Target for Machine Learning: Towards Evaluation and Learning under Democratic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24824)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从哲学层面审视了关于“真实目标（TT）”存在与否的假设转变对机器学习预测建模的影响，提出“民主监督”概念，并构建了基于“多重不精确真实目标（MIATTs）”的评估与学习框架（EL-MIATTs），通过实际应用展示了其在教育和职业发展领域的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流机器学习范式默认假设真实目标（TT）客观存在，这一假设在哲学层面存在局限性。本文旨在通过否定TT的客观存在性，探索新的机器学习视角与范式。

Method: 采用否定本体论视角，明确假设TT在现实世界中并不客观存在；在此基础上定义民主监督（Democratic Supervision）；进一步提出多重不精确真实目标（MIATTs）作为民主监督的实例级实现；基于MIATTs推导逻辑驱动的生成与评估原则、逻辑评估公式以及不可定义真实目标学习机制，最终建立EL-MIATTs框架。

Result: 提出了EL-MIATTs框架，并在真实世界的教育和职业发展应用中展示了其支持个性化发展的潜力。

Conclusion: 否定TT客观存在的哲学假设为机器学习提供了新的视角，民主监督及EL-MIATTs框架在预测建模中具有可行性，尤其在教育和职业发展领域具有应用前景。

Abstract: This article philosophically examines how shifts in assumptions regarding the existence and non-existence of the true target (TT) give rise to new perspectives and insights for machine learning (ML)-based predictive modeling and, correspondingly, proposes a knowledge system for evaluation and learning under Democratic Supervision. By systematically analysing the existence assumption of the TT in current mainstream ML paradigms, we explicitly adopt a negative ontology perspective, positing that the TT does not objectively exist in the real world, and, grounded in this non-existence assumption, define Democratic Supervision for ML. We further present Multiple Inaccurate True Targets (MIATTs) as an instance-level realization of Democratic Supervision. Building upon MIATTs, we derive principles, for the logic-driven generation and assessment of MIATTs, a logical assessment formulation for evaluation with MIATTs, and undefinable true target learning for learning with MIATTs. Based on these components, we establish the evaluation and learning with MIATTs (EL-MIATTs) framework for ML-based predictive modelling. A real-world application demonstrates the potential of the proposed EL-MIATTs framework in supporting education and professional development for individuals, aligning with prior discussions of Democratic Supervision in the fields of education and professional development.

</details>


### [338] [CompleteRXN: Toward Completing Open Chemical Reaction Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00222)
*Gabriel Vogel,Minouk Noordsij,Evgeny Pidko,Jana M. Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompleteRXN基准，用于评估在缺失数据条件下化学反应补全方法。其中CRB模型在随机划分上达99.20%等价准确率，在极端分布外划分上达91.12%，但所有方法在非基准数据上性能显著下降，揭示了基准性能与实际鲁棒性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学反应数据集（如USPTO）存在严重的不完整性，常缺失副产物、共反应物和化学计量系数，限制了其在下游应用中的可靠性与适用性。因此需要建立标准化的基准来评估反应补全方法在真实缺失条件下的表现。

Method: 通过将USPTO记录映射到经过整理的机理反应，构建对齐的不完整与原子平衡反应数据集作为CompleteRXN基准。评估了多种基线方法，包括带约束解码的编码器-解码器反应补全模型CRB、以及算法方法SynRBL。

Result: CRB在随机划分上达到99.20%等价准确率，在极端分布外划分上达91.12%。SynRBL能生成平衡且化学合理的补全，但准确率较低。所有方法随不完整度增加性能下降，且在完整未整理的USPTO数据上性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: CompleteRBN基准上的表现与实际应用之间存在显著差距，需要未来工作在更真实的缺失数据条件下提升反应补全模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Chemical reaction datasets such as USPTO suffer from substantial incompleteness, frequently missing byproducts, co-reactants, and stoichiometric coefficients. This limits their applicability and reliability in downstream applications. Here, we introduce CompleteRXN, a large-scale supervised benchmark for reaction completion under realistic missing-data conditions. We construct a dataset of aligned incomplete and atom-balanced reactions by mapping USPTO records to curated mechanistic reactions. We evaluate representative baselines, including a novel encoder-decoder reaction completion model with constrained decoding, the Constrained Reaction Balancer (CRB), and a recent algorithmic method, SynRBL. On our CompleteRXN benchmark, the CRB achieves high performance across splits of increasing difficulty, reaching 99.20% equivalence accuracy on the random split and 91.12% on the extreme out-of-distribution split. SynRBL produces many balanced and chemically plausible completions, but with lower accuracy on the benchmark test splits. Across all methods, performance degrades with increasing incompleteness. We observe a substantial drop when evaluating on reactions outside the benchmark (full uncurated USPTO), highlighting the gap between benchmark performance and practical robustness and motivating future work.

</details>


### [339] [Towards Efficient and Expressive Offline RL via Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01663)
*Sungyoung Lee,Dohyeong Kim,Eshan Balachandar,Zelal Su Mustafaoglu,Keshav Pingali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning (FAN)，一种高效离线强化学习算法，通过单次流策略迭代和单个高斯噪声样本用于分布性评论家，在显著降低计算成本的同时实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的策略和分布性评论家的离线RL方法性能优异但计算成本高——流策略需要迭代采样生成单个动作，分布性评论家需要多个样本（如分位数）估计价值。FAN旨在解决这些低效问题。

Method: 采用行为正则化技术，仅需单次流策略迭代和单个高斯噪声样本用于分布性评论家；提供了收敛性和性能边界的理论分析。

Result: 在机器人操作和 locomotion 任务上达到最先进性能，同时显著降低训练和推理运行时间。

Conclusion: 证明单次流策略迭代和单噪声样本的简化不仅提升效率，还能带来更优的任务性能。

Abstract: We propose Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning (FAN), a highly efficient and high-performing offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm. Recent work has shown that expressive flow policies and distributional critics improve offline RL performance, but at a high computational cost. Specifically, flow policies require iterative sampling to produce a single action, and distributional critics require computation over multiple samples (e.g., quantiles) to estimate value. To address these inefficiencies while maintaining high performance, we introduce FAN. Our method employs a behavior regularization technique that uses a single flow policy iteration and requires a single Gaussian noise sample for distributional critics. Our theoretical analysis of convergence and performance bounds demonstrates that these simplifications not only improve efficiency but also lead to superior task performance. Experiments on robotic manipulation and locomotion tasks demonstrate that FAN achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing both training and inference runtimes. We release our code at https://github.com/brianlsy98/FAN.

</details>


### [340] [Statistical Consistency and Generalization of Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02116)
*Yuanfan Li,Xiyuan Wei,Tianbao Yang,Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了对比表示学习（CRL）的统一统计学习理论，证明了对比损失在排序任务上的统计一致性，推导了关于负样本数m和锚点数n的泛化界（监督: O(1/m+1/√n)，自监督: O(1/√m+1/√n)），解释了大规模负样本的优越性，并通过大规模视觉-语言模型实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL理论存在三个关键缺陷：统计一致性尚未明确、泛化界随负样本数增加而恶化（与实际经验矛盾）、检索性能缺乏理论分析。本文旨在填补这些空白，建立统一的CRL统计学习理论。

Method: 采用AUC型总体准则评估检索质量，证明对比损失与最优排序统计一致；建立校准型不等式连接对比风险与检索次优性；分别对监督和自监督对比目标推导泛化界，显式刻画负样本数m与锚点数n之间的权衡。

Result: 监督目标泛化界为O(1/m+1/√n)，自监督目标泛化界为O(1/√m+1/√n)，两者均随m增大而改善，解释了大规模负样本的经验优势。在大规模视觉-语言模型上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文为CRL提供了严格的统计学习理论基础，解决了现有理论中泛化界与经验结果矛盾的问题，揭示了m与n的明确权衡关系，并验证了对比损失在排序任务中的统计一致性。

Abstract: Contrastive representation learning (CRL) underpins many modern foundation models. Despite recent theoretical progress, existing analyses suffer from several key limitations: (i) the statistical consistency of CRL remains poorly understood; (ii) available generalization bounds deteriorate as the number of negative samples increases, contradicting the empirical benefits of large negative sets; and (iii) the retrieval performance of CRL has received limited theoretical attention. In this paper, we develop a unified statistical learning theory for CRL. For downstream tasks, we evaluate retrieval quality using an AUC-type population criterion and show that the contrastive loss is \emph{statistically consistent} with optimal ranking. We further establish a \emph{calibration-style inequality} that quantitatively relates excess contrastive risk to excess retrieval suboptimality. For upstream training, we study both supervised and self-supervised contrastive objectives and derive generalization bounds of order $O(1/m + 1/\sqrt{n})$ and $O(1/\sqrt{m} + 1/\sqrt{n})$, respectively, where $m$ denotes the number of negative samples and $n$ the number of anchor points. These bounds not only explain the empirical advantages of large negative sets but also reveal an explicit trade-off between $m$ and $n$. Extensive experiments on large-scale vision--language models corroborate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [341] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了顺序无关自回归模型（Order-Agnostic AR Models），将其置于缺失数据视角下进行分析。研究表明：(1) 全观测数据上的标准训练隐式地在完全随机缺失（MCAR）机制下执行插补，在高缺失率场景下表现稳健；(2) 提出了首个在一般缺失机制下直接使用不完整数据集训练此类模型的理论框架；(3) 利用其摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，即顺序选择最具信息量的缺失变量以辅助下游预测。在多个真实世界基准上，所提出的MO-ARM模型持续优于已有的插补基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但其在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。现有方法缺乏在不完整数据上直接训练的理论框架，也无法在缺失数据场景下进行主动信息选择。

Method: (1) 从理论上证明标准训练隐式等价于MCAR机制下的插补；(2) 提出首个在不完整数据集上直接训练的一般性框架，支持任意缺失机制；(3) 利用模型的摊销条件密度估计能力，设计主动信息获取策略，顺序选择最有助于预测的缺失变量。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上，MO-ARM模型在插补任务中持续优于现有的基线方法。

Conclusion: 顺序无关自回归模型可以有效地推广到缺失数据场景，既能完成稳健的插补，也能进行主动信息获取，为缺失数据下的生成建模提供了新的理论视角和实用框架。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [342] [Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07804)
*Zhicheng Yang,Zhijiang Guo,Yifan Song,Minrui Xu,Yongxin Wang,Yiwei Wang,Xiaodan Liang,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prune-OPD通过动态检测学生与教师模型之间的前缀漂移，实时截断无效轨迹并降低不可靠奖励的权重，从而在维持或提升性能的同时，将训练时间减少37.6%–68.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程任务中扩展开源策略蒸馏（OPD）时，学生生成的前缀不可避免地偏离教师的思维过程，导致教师的密集奖励失去局部可利用性，继续在这些“漂移”轨迹上生成和评估token不仅降低奖励质量，还造成大量计算浪费。

Method: 通过持续监控学生与教师预测之间的局部兼容性（如top-k重叠），实时检测前缀漂移事件；一旦检测到严重漂移，单调降低后续不可靠奖励的权重并触发动态截断，将计算重新分配给可靠的教师监督。

Result: 在多种师生模型组合下，Prune-OPD在出现前缀漂移时将训练时间减少37.6%–68.0%，同时在AMC、AIME、HMMT等挑战性基准上保持甚至提升性能；当兼容性高时，自动扩展训练窗口以保留长上下文监督。

Conclusion: Prune-OPD改进OPD的关键不是盲目缩短轨迹，而是将计算重新分配给局部可利用的教师奖励。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.

</details>


### [343] [PRIM: Meta-Learned Bayesian Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08786)
*Christopher Lohse,Anish Dhir,Amadou Ba,Bradley Eck,Marco Ruffini,Jonas Wahl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIM是一个基于元学习的因果推理方法，将根本原因分析建模为贝叶斯推理任务，通过MACE变压器神经过程在毫秒级实现零样本推理，在合成和真实基准上表现与已知因果图方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的根本原因分析面临三个主要挑战：变量间错误传播、需要结构因果知识、以及测试时推理的计算成本高。现有方法或依赖已知因果图，或计算开销大，难以在实际中高效应用。

Method: PRIM采用因果元学习框架，将RCA建模为在因果模型合成先验上的贝叶斯推理任务。通过边缘化结构不确定性，隐式识别基线期与异常期数据生成机制的变化。核心是MACE（Model-Averaged Causal Estimation）变压器神经过程，联合关注观测样本、异常样本和节点因果结构，无需测试时模型拟合即可实现零样本推理。

Result: 在合成基准和两个真实基准数据集PetShop和CausRCA上，PRIM与已知系统因果图的方法竞争力相当，在多项任务上优于不知因果图的方法。处理100个变量的系统只需17毫秒。轻量级微调可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: PRIM通过元学习隐式学习因果结构，避免了测试时结构推断的开销，在不牺牲准确性的前提下实现了高效的根本原因分析，为实际部署提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in complex systems is challenging due to error propagation across multiple variables, the need for structural causal knowledge, and the computational cost of inference at test time. We introduce PRIM (Prior-fitted Root cause Identification with Meta-learning), a causal meta-learning approach that frames RCA as a Bayesian inference task over a synthetic prior of causal models. By marginalising out structural uncertainty, PRIM implicitly identifies changes in the data-generating mechanism between baseline and anomalous periods. In doing so, PRIM infers distributional differences without explicit statistical testing, and implicitly learns causal structure without model fitting at test time. Following the simulation-based meta-learning paradigm of prior-fitted networks, PRIM uses a Model-Averaged Causal Estimation (MACE) transformer neural process that jointly attends over observational and anomalous samples and the causal structure of nodes, enabling zero-shot inference in 17,ms for systems with up to 100 variables. Across synthetic benchmarks and two realistic benchmark datasets, PetShop and CausRCA, PRIM is competitive with methods that are aware of the system's causal graphical structure a priori while outperforming graph-unaware methods on several tasks. Lightweight fine-tuning to specific domains and data dynamics improves performance further.

</details>


### [344] [TopoGeoScore: A Self-Supervised Source-Only Geometric Framework for OOD Checkpoint Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08870)
*Farid Hazratian,Ali Zia,Hien Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TopoGeoScore，一种仅使用源域表示进行无标签OOD鲁棒性检查点选择的方法，通过类条件互kNN图中的拓扑与几何信号（扭转Laplacian行列式、Ollivier-Ricci曲率、高阶拓扑摘要）以及自监督学习权重，无需目标域样本即可评估鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标域标签不可用时，难以诊断OOD鲁棒性；现有无监督精度估计方法通常需要目标样本，而更严格的源域仅变体（无目标样本、无目标标签）尚缺乏有效的方案。

Method: 构建类条件互kNN图，提取三类可解释信号：扭转Laplacian对数行列式（全局流形复杂度）、Ollivier-Ricci曲率（局部邻域规则性）、高阶拓扑摘要（碎片连接、环路、全局-局部不一致性）；通过自监督目标学习非负线性权重，该目标在近似保几何的嵌入视图下保持不变性并与破坏结构的视图分离。

Result: 在CIFAR类损坏与分布偏移基准、ImageNet-C、MNLI→HANS迁移、OGBN-Arxiv上的实验表明，源域表示中包含可测量的全局-局部-拓扑鲁棒性证据，支持部署前的实用检查点选择。

Conclusion: 源域表示本身蕴含可量化的几何与拓扑鲁棒性信号，TopoGeoScore能够在不使用任何目标域样本或标签的情况下实现有效的鲁棒检查点选择。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness is difficult to diagnose when target-domain labels are unavailable. We consider a more restrictive source-only variant of unsupervised accuracy estimation: selecting robust checkpoints using only source-domain representations, with no target samples or target labels. We propose \textbf{TopoGeoScore}, a source-only geometric scorer for label-free OOD checkpoint selection. Given a trained checkpoint, we construct class-conditional mutual $k$-nearest-neighbour graphs from source embeddings and extract three interpretable signals: a torsion-inspired reduced Laplacian log-determinant for global class-manifold complexity, Ollivier--Ricci curvature for local neighbourhood regularity, and higher-order topological summaries for fragmented connectivity, loops, and global--local inconsistency. Instead of fixing their weights by hand, TopoGeoScore learns a non-negative linear score through a self-supervised objective that enforces invariance under approximately geometry-preserving embedding views and separation from structure-breaking views. The score remains interpretable and uses no target-domain samples or labels. Results across CIFAR-based corruption and distribution-shift benchmarks, ImageNet-C, MNLI$\to$HANS transfer, and OGBN-Arxiv suggest that source representations contain measurable global--local--topological evidence of robustness, supporting practical checkpoint selection before deployment under distribution shift.

</details>


### [345] [Nearly-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial Kernelized Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10299)
*Shogo Iwazaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗环境下的核化赌博机问题，提出了基于指数权重的算法，实现了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$ 的对抗遗憾界，并针对SE和Matérn核给出了算法无关的下界以证明最优性，同时利用Nyström近似提出了计算高效的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核化赌博机研究主要针对随机环境，而现实场景中奖励可能被对抗性地选择，因此需要研究对抗环境下的核化赌博机算法及其理论保证。

Method: 采用指数权重算法（exponential-weight algorithm），并结合Nyström近似提出计算高效的变体。

Result: 达到了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$ 的对抗遗憾界；对于SE和Matérn核，给出了算法无关的下界，证明算法在多项式对数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 所提算法在对抗环境下实现了近乎最优的遗憾界，且可通过Nyström近似实现高效计算。

Abstract: This paper studies kernelized bandits (also known as Gaussian process bandits) in an adversarial environment, where the reward functions in a known reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) may be adversarially chosen at each round. We show that the exponential-weight algorithm achieves $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$ adversarial regret, where $T$ and $γ_T$ denote the number of total rounds and the maximum information gain, respectively. For squared exponential (SE) and $ν$-Matérn kernels, we also show algorithm-independent lower bounds that guarantee the optimality of our algorithm up to polylogarithmic factors. Furthermore, we present a computationally efficient variant of our algorithm using Nyström approximation while maintaining nearly optimal regret guarantees.

</details>


### [346] [Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization for On-Policy Reasoning Distillation under Large Policy Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13230)
*Xinyu Liu,Kechen Jiao,Chunyang Xiao,Runsong Zhao,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Jiahao Liu,Qifan Wang,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Chenglong Wang,Tong Xiao,JingBo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法在师生策略差异大时因学生轨迹超出教师分布导致监督失效的问题，提出TGPO方法，通过教师直接指导token级生成并结合RLVR奖励，在推理基准上持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法依赖基于逆KL（RKL）的教师监督，但当师生策略差异较大时，RL驱动的探索产生的轨迹往往超出教师分布，导致负反馈信息不足，限制了OPD在推理场景下的有效性。

Method: TGPO包含两个核心组件：1) 教师直接提供基于学生生成上下文的token级生成指导（而非仅评价性监督）；2) 结合RLVR风格的轨迹级奖励，协同引导学生探索更优的推理延续。

Result: 在推理基准测试上，TGPO一致优于现有基于RKL的OPD方法，并且在不同的教师模型下均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TGPO通过引入教师对学生上下文的细粒度生成指导，有效解决了大策略差异下OPD失效的问题，是一种更鲁棒有效的推理蒸馏方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a promising paradigm for reasoning-oriented post-training of large language models (LLMs), especially when combined with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR). Existing OPD methods rely on reverse KL (RKL)-based teacher supervision over trajectories sampled from the student policy. However, we identify a critical limitation: under large teacher--student policy divergence, RL-driven exploration often produces trajectories outside the teacher distribution, resulting in uninformative negative feedback. To address this, we propose Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO), an on-policy reasoning distillation method that remains effective under large policy divergence settings. Rather than relying solely on evaluative supervision, TGPO uses teacher to directly guide token level generation conditioning on student-generated contexts; together with RLVR-style trajectory level rewards, TGPO steers exploration toward improved continuations. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that TGPO consistently outperforms existing RKL-based OPD methods and remains robust across different teacher models.

</details>


### [347] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个面向表格数据的基座模型，通过大规模扩展（支持100万训练行）、优化训练/推理速度、引入测试时计算扩展，在TabArena等基准测试中显著超越所有现有模型，同时保持10-20倍的速度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tabular数据支撑着科学和工业中最具价值的大多数预测问题，而之前的TabPFN虽已推动该领域的基座模型革命，但仍存在性能天花板和效率瓶颈。用户反馈驱动下，作者希望扩展到百万级数据集、大幅降低训练和推理时间，并拓展到时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据等更广泛的应用场景。

Method: 1. 全程使用来自先验分布的合成数据进行预训练；2. 引入测试时计算扩展（Test-Time Compute Scaling）；3. 通过减少KV缓存和行分块技术（row-chunking）扩展至100万行数据；4. 提供专门的时间序列检查点TabPFN-TS-3；5. 提供API版本TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking)利用测试时计算扩展进一步提升性能。

Result: 在TabArena基准上，TabPFN-3前向传播显著超越所有模型（包括经过调优和集成的基线）；在多样性数据集上，对多类别数据集排名第一，在100万训练行和200特征的数据集上击败8小时调优的梯度提升树基线；TabPFN-3-Plus在TabArena上超越所有非TabPFN模型200+ Elo，最大数据子集上达420 Elo，比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍；在RelBenchV1上实现新SOTA；TabPFN-3-Plus在TabSTAR上实现SOTA；TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench上排名第二；SHAP值计算速度提升120倍；整体速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3显著推动了表格预测的前沿，在性能、速度和可扩展性上均实现了重大突破，同时证明了合成数据预训练和测试时计算扩展在表格基座模型中的有效性，且不依赖LLM、真实数据或互联网搜索。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [348] [Latency-Quality Routing for Functionally Equivalent Tools in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14241)
*Kexin Chu,Dawei Xiang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LQM-ContextRoute，一个面向同功能工具提供商的上下文赌博机路由器。其核心设计是延迟-质量匹配机制：将延迟视为服务容量而非抵消奖励，结合查询特定质量估计和LLM评判反馈，实现在线自适应路由。在多个基准上相比SW-UCB提升F1达+2.18pp、准确率最高+18pp、NDCG提升+2.91~+3.22pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型LLM智能体越来越多地通过多个功能等价但性能（延迟、可靠性、答案质量）不同的提供商访问同一工具类型，产生了运行时负载下的提供商路由问题。现有方法缺乏部署时的真实标签，且将延迟与质量在加性奖励中直接折衷会导致次优路由决策。

Method: 提出LQM-ContextRoute上下文赌博机路由器。关键设计是延迟-质量匹配（Latency-Quality Matching）：按每个服务周期内的预期答案质量对提供商排序，而非让低延迟抵消低质量的加性奖励。结合容量感知评分、查询特定质量估计和LLM-as-Judge在线反馈，实现对负载变化和提供商质量差异的自适应。

Result: 在Web搜索负载基准上，F1比SW-UCB提升+2.18pp，且处于延迟-质量前沿。在异构性高的StrategyQA设定中，避免加性奖励崩溃，准确率比SW-UCB最高提升+18pp。在异构检索器池上，NDCG比SW-UCB提升+2.91~+3.22pp。

Conclusion: 同功能工具路由应将延迟视为服务容量而非质量折衷因子，尤其当运行时压力与提供商质量异构性并存时。延迟-质量匹配设计显著优于传统加性奖励方法。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents increasingly access the same tool type through multiple functionally equivalent providers, such as web-search APIs, retrievers, or LLM backends exposed behind a shared interface. This creates a provider-routing problem under runtime load: the router must choose among providers that differ in latency, reliability, and answer quality, often without gold labels at deployment time. We introduce LQM-ContextRoute, a contextual bandit router for same-function tool providers. Its key design is latency-quality matching: instead of letting low latency offset poor answers in an additive reward, the router ranks providers by expected answer quality per service cycle. It combines this capacity-aware score with query-specific quality estimation and LLM-as-judge feedback, allowing it to adapt online to both load changes and provider-quality differences. On the main web-search load benchmark, LQM-ContextRoute improves F1 by +2.18 pp over SW-UCB while staying on the latency-quality frontier. In a high-heterogeneity StrategyQA setting, LQM-ContextRoute avoids additive-reward collapse and improves accuracy by up to +18 pp over SW-UCB; on heterogeneous retriever pools, it improves NDCG by +2.91--+3.22 pp over SW-UCB. These results show that same-function tool routing benefits from treating latency as service capacity, especially when runtime pressure and provider-quality heterogeneity coexist.

</details>


### [349] [Turning Stale Gradients into Stable Gradients: Coherent Coordinate Descent with Implicit Landscape Smoothing for Lightweight Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14373)
*Chen Liang,Xiatao Sun,Qian Wang,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD)，一种确定性、样本高效且预算感知的零阶优化器。通过将历史梯度从负担转化为计算资产，实现每步O(1)查询复杂度，并发现大步长有限差分可产生隐式平滑效应，在MLP/CNN/ResNet上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法在样本效率与方差之间存在根本性权衡：标准有限差分样本效率低，随机子空间方法方差高。需要一种既样本高效又低方差的确定性方法。

Method: 提出Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD)，一种确定性的、样本高效且预算感知的零阶优化器。理论形式化梯度一致性概念，证明CoCD等价于带"热启动"的块循环坐标下降（BCCD），将历史（陈旧）梯度转化为计算资产。

Result: 1) 实现每步O(1)查询复杂度，同时保持全局下降方向。2) 推导误差界发现反直觉结论：更大的有限差分步长可通过减小有效光滑常数产生隐式平滑效应。3) 在MLP、CNN、ResNet（高达27万参数）上，CoCD在样本效率和收敛损失/准确率上显著优于BCCD，且比随机化ZO方法更稳定。

Conclusion: 确定性的、结构感知的更新是轻量级零阶优化中优于随机化的替代方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization is pivotal for scenarios where backpropagation is unavailable, such as memory-constrained on-device learning and black-box optimization. However, existing methods face a stark trade-off: they are either sample-inefficient (e.g., standard finite differences) or suffer from high variance due to randomized estimation (e.g., random subspace methods). In this work, we propose Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD), a deterministic, sample-efficient, and budget-aware ZO optimizer. Theoretically, we formalize the notion of gradient coherence and demonstrate that CoCD is equivalent to Block Cyclic Coordinate Descent (BCCD) with ``warm starts,'' effectively converting historical (stale) gradients from a liability into a computational asset. This mechanism enables $O(1)$ query complexity per step while maintaining global descent directions. Furthermore, we derive error bounds revealing a counter-intuitive insight: larger finite-difference step sizes can induce an implicit smoothing effect on the optimization landscape by reducing the effective smoothness constant, thereby improving convergence stability. Experiments on MLP, CNN, and ResNet architectures (up to 270k parameters) demonstrate that CoCD significantly outperforms BCCD in terms of sample efficiency and convergence loss/accuracy, and exhibits superior stability over randomized ZO methods. Our results suggest that deterministic, structure-aware updates offer a superior alternative to randomization for lightweight ZO optimization.

</details>


### [350] [To MRL or not to MRL: Text Embeddings are Robust to Truncation Without Matryoshka Learning, Except In Heavy Truncation Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16608)
*Sotaro Takeshita,Yurina Takeshita,Simone Paolo Ponzetto,Daniel Ruffinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了MRL训练与无MRL训练下向量截断的效果，发现除非截断比例超过80%，否则普通模型的截断嵌入与MRL模型性能相当甚至更优，表明截断鲁棒性未必来自MRL训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明随机截断文本嵌入对下游性能影响很小，但尚无工作系统比较随机截断与MRL方法的效果差异，因此本文旨在厘清两种嵌入压缩方法的实际性能对比。

Method: 将MRL中使用的同一种截断方式分别应用于有MRL训练和无MRL训练的模型，在多个模型和下游任务上进行性能对比实验。

Result: 除非嵌入向量被截断80%以上，否则无MRL模型的截断嵌入在性能上与MRL模型相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: 截断鲁棒性可能并非来自MRL训练本身；是否采用MRL的额外训练开销取决于是否需要对嵌入进行高度截断（≥80%）。

Abstract: Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) is a widely adopted approach for training text encoders so they provide useful text representations at various sizes, available by simply truncating the resulting vectors at sizes pre-determined at training time. Recent works have shown that randomly truncating text embeddings has minimal impact in downstream performance unless vectors are reduced in size by at least 70%, suggesting that embeddings are already robust to truncation without the use of MRL. However, no prior work has compared random truncation to MRL, so it is unclear how the two methods compare as effective embedding reduction methods. In this paper, we study this by applying the same truncation used by MRL to models trained with and without MRL. Our results across several models and downstream tasks show that, unless heavily truncating embeddings (i.e. reducing their size by at least 80%), truncated embeddings of non-MRL models are competitive with, and often outperform models trained with MRL. This suggests that truncation robustness may not necessarily come from MRL, and that the choice of spending the additional training cost of MRL depends on whether heavy truncation is desired. We make our code available for reproduction.

</details>


### [351] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM)，通过嵌套层次化概念组织实现单模型内的自适应概念利用，将预期干预成本从线性降至对数级别O(log K)，并保证单调性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）存在结构性局限：要么使用单一静态概念集，导致专家需要穷举标注概念，干预成本高昂；要么训练多个不同概念预算的模型，带来大量计算和维护开销。亟需一种能动态适配不同概念预算的统一模型架构。

Method: 受Matryoshka表示学习启发，MCBM将概念按最大相关性和最小冗余度组织成嵌套层次结构，在不重新训练的情况下支持多粒度概念推理。通过这种嵌套结构实现从线性到对数级别的干预成本降低。

Result: 理论证明MCBM将预期干预成本从O(K)降至O(log K)；实验表明MCBM在匹配独立训练模型性能的同时，实现了动态高效的专家交互。

Conclusion: MCBM通过单模型内的层次化概念结构，解决了CBM部署中干预成本高的问题，为可解释深度学习提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [352] [A Tutorial on Diffusion Theory: From Differential Equations to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22586)
*Jiayi Fu,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从微分方程视角统一阐述了扩散模型的数学基础，包括ODE/SDE表示、逆时采样、分数匹配等核心概念，并将DDPM、DDIM、流匹配和分数SDE置于同一框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的数学基础分散在多种框架中（扩散概率模型、分数建模、随机微分方程、数值采样方法），缺乏统一的自洽阐述，给研究者和学习者带来理解障碍。

Method: 从条件高斯加噪过程出发，推导ODE和SDE表示，建立正向边际动力学，进而得到逆时SDE和概率流ODE；通过分数匹配推导去噪目标，讨论逆时采样和引导策略；将DDPM、DDIM、流匹配、分数SDE统一在同一框架下，并扩展到连续嵌入空间的扩散语言模型和离散掩码token扩散。

Result: 建立了扩散模型不同视角（扩散概率模型、分数建模、SDE、数值采样）的统一数学框架，阐明了逆时采样中核心未知量为边际分数，分数匹配在噪声预测参数化下即为标准去噪目标。

Conclusion: 该教程作为扩散过程分析基础与现代生成算法之间的桥梁，提供了统一且自洽的数学理解。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a dominant framework for generative modeling, but their mathematical foundations are often presented separately through diffusion probabilistic models, score-based modeling, stochastic differential equations, and numerical sampling methods. We write this tutorial to provide a unified and self-contained account of these viewpoints from the perspective of differential equations. Starting from a conditional Gaussian noising process, we derive ordinary differential equation (ODE) and stochastic differential equation (SDE) representations, pass to the corresponding marginal forward dynamics, and then obtain the reverse-time SDE and probability-flow ODE that make generation possible. We show that the central unknown quantity in reverse sampling is the marginal score, explain how score matching becomes the standard denoising objective under a noise-prediction parameterization, and discuss practical reverse-time sampling and guidance. We further place DDPM, DDIM, flow matching, and score-based SDEs in a common framework, and conclude with diffusion language models in continuous embedding space together with a brief discussion of discrete masked-token diffusion. The tutorial is intended as a bridge between the analytical foundations of diffusion processes and the modern generative algorithms built upon them.

</details>


### [353] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段联邦推荐系统流水线，将非敏感用户偏好数据与敏感移动上下文数据分离，第一阶段在云端协同过滤生成候选列表，第二阶段在设备端利用敏感信号重排序，仅传输模型梯度/更新，并在多个数据集上验证，提供了生产级Kotlin跨平台实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式服务个性化内容需要汇集用户敏感数据到中央服务器，这种做法日益与隐私期望和地区法规相冲突，因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能实现个性化推荐的解决方案。

Method: 两阶段联邦推荐系统：第一阶段在云端基于非敏感应用上下文数据运行协同过滤模型生成候选短列表；第二阶段在设备端利用敏感移动信号（从不离设备）对候选进行重排序，仅模型更新/梯度离开设备。

Result: 在MovieLens、UCI Human Activity Recognition和专有试点数据集上验证了该方法，并交付了支持Android和iOS的生产级Kotlin Multiplatform库实现。

Conclusion: 通过原则性的数据分离和联邦学习机制，实现了在不暴露敏感移动上下文数据的前提下提供个性化推荐，兼顾了隐私保护与推荐质量。

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [354] [Tiny Brains, Giant Impact: Uncovering the Keystone Neurons of LLM with Just a Few Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24846)
*Xiangtian Ji,Yuxin Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM中存在一类跨任务持续高度激活的"基石神经元"（keystone neurons），移除它们会导致模型能力崩溃。这些神经元在预训练中形成，参数校准对模型能力至关重要。基于此提出仅更新基石神经元的SFT方法，在少量参数修改下达到甚至超越全参数微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM展现出强大的综合能力，但其内部工作机制尚不清晰，缺乏对支撑模型行为的核心神经元的系统识别和理解。

Method: 1) 跨多能力维度任务识别持续高激活的神经元子集；2) 通过探测跨任务激活强度，隔离出极其稀疏的基石神经元；3) 分析基石神经元的稳定性和内在性；4) 提出仅更新基石神经元的SFT方法。

Result: 移除基石神经元会导致模型行为崩溃；这些神经元在预训练中稳定形成，其参数校准对模型能力至关重要；仅更新基石神经元的SFT方法在修改极少参数的情况下，任务性能达到甚至超越全参数微调，同时更好地保留其他能力维度。

Conclusion: LLM中存在关键的基石神经元子集，它们是模型能力的核心支撑，针对性地微调这些神经元可以在保持模型通用能力的同时高效提升特定任务性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) display strong comprehensive abilities, yet the internal mechanisms that support these behaviors remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we show that across a wide range of open-weight Transformers, a subset of neurons remains consistently highly activated during inference across tasks of multiple capability dimensions. By probing along the cross-task activation strength, an extremely sparse subset is isolated, whose removal causes a collapse in model behavior, which we term keystone neurons. Our analysis reveals that keystone neurons are a stable and intrinsic neuron subset of the model that is largely established during pretraining. The parameters associated with these neurons are tightly calibrated during the training process, and their precise values are critical for the capabilities of the model. Building on these insights, we propose a supervised fine-tuning approach that updates only keystone neurons, achieving task gains comparable to or even better than full-parameter fine-tuning while better preserving performance in other capability dimensions, despite modifying a much smaller number of parameters.

</details>


### [355] [Theoretical Analysis of Sparse Optimization with Reparameterization, Weight Decay, and Adaptive Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25134)
*Huangyu Xu,Jingqin Yang,Qianqian Xu,Jiaye Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReWA方法，通过重参数化、权重衰减和自适应学习率解决$\ell_p$正则化（0<p<1）在稀疏优化中的梯度不稳定问题，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上相比$\ell_1$正则化显著提升稀疏性且不损失测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\ell_p$正则化（0<p<1）虽能促进稀疏性，但存在梯度无界导致的优化不稳定问题，亟需一种既能保持稀疏性又能稳定优化的方法。

Method: 提出ReWA方法，结合重参数化（Reparameterization）、权重衰减（Weight decay）和自适应学习率（Adaptive learning rate），构建与$\ell_p$正则化紧密相关但具有更优优化景观的替代方案。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上使用ResNet架构，ReWA相比$\ell_1$正则化显著提升了模型稀疏性，同时保持了测试精度。

Conclusion: ReWA通过改变优化景观有效缓解了$\ell_p$正则化的不稳定问题，为稀疏优化提供了一种更稳定且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Sparse optimization is a fundamental challenge in various practical applications. A popular approach to sparse optimization is $\ell_p$ regularization. However, it may encounter optimization instability due to the unbounded gradients when $0<p<1$. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach to sparse optimization termed ReWA, based on Reparameterization, Weight decay, and Adaptive learning rate. ReWA is closely connected to $\ell_p$-regularization, yet it unveils a distinct optimization landscape that helps mitigate instability issues. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet with ResNets demonstrate that ReWA leads to significant sparsity improvements over the $\ell_1$-regularization approach while preserving test accuracy.

</details>


### [356] [Autoregression-Free Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25413)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Caiyan Qin,Haoyu Bian,Libin Cai,Yi Lu,Chaoning Zhang,Wei Dong,Yuanfang Guo,Yang Yang,Hen Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自回归自由神经算子(AFNO)，将PDE时间演化映射到潜空间，利用流匹配学习连续向量场，避免自回归滚动，实现稳定长程预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经算子的方法处理含时PDE时依赖自回归滚动直接在高维物理场空间预测，导致长程预测中误差累积严重，且缺乏连续时间建模能力。

Method: 将PDE时间演化映射到潜空间，在该空间中利用流匹配学习连续时间向量场，通过显式物理参数条件化捕捉不同参数配置下的动力学行为，完全避免自回归滚动。

Result: 在6个PDE基准上，AFNO相比基线方法显著提升了长程预测稳定性，并一致降低了滚动误差。

Conclusion: 潜空间连续时间建模结合流匹配是突破神经算子长程预测瓶颈的有效途径，避免了传统自回归策略的误差累积问题。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings from function-dependent inputs to solutions, providing an effective framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). For time-dependent PDEs, existing methods typically perform long-horizon prediction through autoregressive rollout directly in high-dimensional physical field spaces, where each predicted state is recursively fed back as the input for the next step. Although effective for short-term prediction, this autoregressive rollout and the lack of continuous-time modeling lead to progressive error accumulation over long-horizon rollouts. In this work, we propose Autoregression-Free Neural Operators (AFNO), which map the time evolution of PDEs into a latent space and model continuous-time vector fields within it. AFNO uses flow matching to learn the latent vector field, thereby enabling continuous evolution over extended horizons, avoiding autoregressive rollout and capturing dynamics under varying parameter configurations through explicit conditioning on physical parameters. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments on six PDEs demonstrate that AFNO improves long-horizon prediction stability and consistently reduces rollout errors compared with the baselines.

</details>


### [357] [GoQuant: Geometric Orthogonal Residual Projection for Multiplier-Free Power-of-Two Transformer Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26092)
*Maoyang Xiang,Bo Wang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GoQuant，一种算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过几何正交残差投影量化方法，在超低位宽（<4-bit）下替代传统MAC运算为移位加操作，解决对数Power-of-Two量化的低角度分辨率问题，在LLaMA-2-7B 3-bit下达到6.10困惑度，且关键路径延迟降至0.35ns。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和ViT在边缘设备部署受限于内存限制和密集MAC阵列带来的时序瓶颈。超低位宽下PoT量化虽可用移位替代MAC，但其非均匀指数格点存在低角度分辨率结构缺陷，导致高维特征流形退化。

Method: 提出几何正交残差投影量化(GoQuant)，将量化形式化为双基几何投影，自适应合成高分辨率残差格点；采用移位加操作替代MAC；使用解析求解器替代梯度优化，将LLaMA-2-7B全模型校准时间降至约15分钟。

Result: 3-bit (W3/A16)下LLaMA-2-7B困惑度6.10，与依赖非对称缩放的AWQ等MAC密集型基线相当；28nm节点RTL综合显示并行移位加数据路径将关键路径延迟降至0.35ns。

Conclusion: GoQuant在超低位宽量化中有效缓解了低角度分辨率问题，无需非对称缩放即可在3-4 bit下保持竞争力，同时通过消除密集乘法树显著改善硬件时序瓶颈，适用于跨模态部署。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) on edge devices is significantly constrained by memory limitations and the critical timing bottlenecks introduced by dense Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) arrays. In the ultra-low bit regime, logarithmic Power-of-Two (PoT) quantization provides a hardware-efficient alternative by replacing MAC operations with bit-shifts. However, the non-uniform exponential lattice is inherently limited by a \textbf{Low Angular Resolution Regime}, a structural flaw that becomes particularly pronounced at sub-4-bit thresholds, leading to a notable degradation of high-dimensional feature manifolds. To address this geometric limitation, we propose Geometric Orthogonal Residual Projection Quantization (GoQuant), an algorithm-hardware co-design framework. By formulating quantization as a dual-basis geometric projection, GoQuant adaptively synthesizes a higher-resolution residual lattice using strictly shift-and-add operations. Furthermore, its analytical solver offers a practical alternative to computationally intensive gradient-based optimization, reducing the full-model calibration time for LLaMA-2-7B to approximately 15 minutes. Extensive evaluations demonstrate GoQuant's applicability across modalities and its hardware efficiency. Under the 3-bit (W3/A16) constraint, it achieves a perplexity of 6.10 on LLaMA-2-7B, comparing favorably to conventional MAC-intensive baselines like AWQ without relying on asymmetric scaling, while maintaining competitive accuracy in 4-bit scenarios. At the silicon level, standard-cell RTL synthesis at a 28nm node indicates that GoQuant effectively mitigates the timing bottlenecks associated with dense multiplier trees. By flattening the combinational logic depth, our parallel shift-and-add datapath reduces the critical path delay to 0.35 ns.

</details>


### [358] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAD框架，通过统一分类范式（Outlier Exposure）和重建范式（Masked Autoencoder）的互补优势，利用分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码辅助重建模块，同时缓解分类模块的泛化问题，实现了对微妙和复杂时间序列异常的有效检测，在严格评估协议下显著超越现有方法，且轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测中，基于Outlier Exposure的分类方法泛化能力差，基于Masked Autoencoder的重建方法存在掩码对齐问题，两者各自受限，亟需一种能发挥两者互补优势的统一框架。

Method: 提出CoAD框架，核心是分类模块与重建模块的协作设计：分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码传递给重建模块，重建模块反过来缓解分类模块的泛化问题；同时分类模块针对分类粒度不当和频率信息忽视问题进行了专门设计。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，采用严格评估协议，CoAD显著超越现有深度学习和传统数据挖掘方法，且模型轻量、推理速度远超现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 通过统一分类与重建两种范式，CoAD有效解决了各自局限性，展现了深度学习在时间序列异常检测中的潜力，并具备大规模实时应用的实际价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [359] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathoFM提出面向脊髓损伤病理步态分析的编码器中心Transformer，通过局部补全、时间连续性和无监督上下文动态三种互补预训练目标，发现动态中心的混合目标在分类与回归任务间实现最佳迁移平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习受限于小规模异质队列和协议漂移，而下游任务同时涵盖分类（病理诊断）和回归（时序预测）。这种双重约束使得基础模型预训练具有吸引力，但关键问题在于：预训练目标应施加何种归纳偏置，才能使表示在任务类型和受试者间有效迁移。

Method: 提出PathoFM框架，使用编码器中心Transformer在多元步态窗口上预训练，采用三种互补目标：(1)局部补全——重建连续掩码片段以强制局部结构；(2)时间连续性——从观测前缀预测掩码的中期延续以强制平滑性和因果一致性；(3)无监督上下文动态——通过注意力机制以受试者示例窗口为条件进行支持-查询重建。经验性比较了分组/对比、基于动态和生成式重建三类目标族。

Result: 动态中心的目标混合产生最平衡的迁移效果：分组目标有利于判别边界但损害连续目标所需的量值保真度，而纯重建目标保留波形结构但分类性能可能不足。结合局部重建与时间连续性，并在可获取示例时添加上下文条件，可产生鲁棒的受试者泛化表示。

Conclusion: 对于脊髓损伤病理步态分析，动态中心的预训练目标混合（局部补全+时间连续性+上下文动态）在分类与回归任务间实现了最优的迁移平衡。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [360] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了非线性时间序列因果发现中使用标量边分数会严重损失信息，提出应使用函数值因果影响分析，并通过神经加性向量自回归模型和个体条件期望框架，展示标量分数无法区分的边在函数行为上存在质的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性时序因果发现方法几乎全部使用标量边分数总结因果关系，但这种做法掩盖了非线性模型实际学习到的状态依赖函数——其效应随机制、幅度和背景而变化，无法有效区分状态间变异和残差噪声。

Method: 形式化定义了可加贡献分解架构下的函数值因果影响；以神经加性向量自回归为代表性架构，基于个体条件期望构建从训练模型中估计因果响应函数的实用框架；通过合成实验和民主发展案例进行验证。

Result: 标量分数无法区分的边在函数行为上表现出定性差异，包括单调、阈值、饱和及符号翻转效应；在民主发展案例中，函数值分析揭示了分数中心方法系统遗漏的特定机制和非对称因果结构。

Conclusion: 标量因果分数构成严重信息瓶颈，函数值因果影响分析能揭示更丰富的因果结构，应成为非线性时序因果发现的必要补充。

Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

</details>


### [361] [Two Speeds of Learning: A Representation-Readout Decomposition of Grokking and Double Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27078)
*Chi-Ning Chou,Oscar Uzdelewicz,Neng-Chun Chiu,Yao-Yuan Yang,SueYeon Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征-读出分解框架，通过分析编码器中的表征学习和最终分类器中的读出校准这两个竞争过程，统一解释了深度学习中的grokking（突现泛化）和epoch-wise double descent（逐轮双下降）等异常泛化现象。该框架在多种任务和架构上有效，并提供了区分真正泛化与虚假泛化的诊断特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对grokking和double descent等异常泛化现象的解释往往针对特定任务，缺乏一个跨任务和架构的通用分析框架。作者旨在建立一个任务无关的顶层框架来诊断和解释这些现象。

Method: 利用表征几何、神经正切核（NTK）和线性探针（linear probing）等工具，将学习动态分解为编码器的表征学习和分类器的读出校准两个竞争过程，并分析两者相对速度的波动如何导致异常泛化行为。

Result: 1) 在grokking中，grokking发生前读出偏向训练集，表征学习虽缓慢但并未缺失（反驳了lazy-to-rich观点）；2) 框架能区分真正泛化与虚假泛化（如MNIST grokking和epoch-wise double descent中，异常泛化源于表征退化与读出错位）；3) 该框架在多种任务和架构上均有效。

Conclusion: 表征-读出分解可作为理解学习动态和揭示底层算法的通用顶层框架，对可解释性研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Training loss and accuracy are the standard signals used to monitor generalization during deep neural network training. Two well-documented phenomena complicate this picture: in grokking, train loss falls rapidly while test performance improves abruptly only after a long delay; in epoch-wise double descent, train loss decreases monotonically while test loss or error rises and falls. Existing accounts are often task-specific, and a task-agnostic analysis framework for diagnosing and explaining these phenomena across realistic tasks and architectures is missing. We address this challenge by analyzing two competing processes that underlie learning dynamics: representation learning in the encoder and readout calibration in the final classifier. Using tools from representational geometry, neural tangent kernels, and linear probing, we show that both processes are active throughout training, with the fluctuations of their relative speed giving rise to seemingly anomalous generalization dynamics. Applying the representation-readout decomposition to grokking across a wide range of tasks and architectures, we find that the readout is train-biased before grokking onset, and representation learning is gradual but not absent, contrary to the lazy-to-rich account. The framework further provides diagnostic signatures distinguishing spurious from genuine generalization: in a previously reported MNIST grokking example and an epoch-wise double descent example, apparent delayed or non-monotone generalization is shown to arise from representation degradation and readout misalignment induced by non-standard training recipes. Together, these results establish the representation-readout decomposition as a top-down framework for understanding learning dynamics and revealing underlying algorithms for interpretability research.

</details>


### [362] [Density-aware Sample-specific Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27809)
*Qiyuan Wang,Yao Li,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低密度区域投毒的最优后门攻击方法，通过条件时间分数匹配估计密度比，并利用双层优化将触发样本置于干净数据分布的稀疏区域，在Fine-tuning防御下保持50-85%的攻击成功率，对神经元剪枝防御完全免疫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击方法在面临微调或剪枝等训练后防御时容易被擦除后门，缺乏能够抵御这些防御手段的鲁棒攻击方法。

Method: 1. 从贝叶斯最优训练模型出发，推导出最优样本特定触发器构造的准则；2. 提出将触发样本导向干净数据分布的低密度区域，该条件同时控制被污染分布的所有矩；3. 设计双层优化框架，通过条件时间分数匹配估计密度比，并优化混合模型目标函数将触发样本置于稀疏区域。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB和TinyImageNet上，攻击成功率在防御前超过99%，在微调防御下比最强基线高出50-85个百分点，对神经元剪枝防御具有完全免疫性（零神经元被识别移除）。

Conclusion: 当前防御范式存在根本性缺陷，需要设计能够超越干净数据分布支撑集的防御机制。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in backdoor attacks, existing methods remain susceptible to post-training defenses that erase the backdoor through fine-tuning or pruning. We revisit the core objectives of backdoor attacks and derive principled criteria characterizing optimal sample-specific trigger construction under a Bayes-optimal model of the victim's training. Our analysis reveals that both attack success and clean-accuracy preservation are simultaneously optimized when triggered samples are steered into low-density regions of the clean data distribution, a distributional condition that controls all moments of the poisoned distribution at once rather than a handful of input-space summary statistics. We introduce a bilevel optimization framework that estimates density ratios via conditional time-score matching and optimizes a mixture-model objective to place triggered samples in these sparse regions. Extensive evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet demonstrate that our method achieves above 99\% attack success rate before defense and retains 50--85 percentage points higher post-defense ASR than the strongest baselines under fine-tuning defenses. Against neuron-pruning defenses, the method exhibits complete immunity, with zero neurons identified for removal across all pruning thresholds. These results expose a fundamental gap in current defense paradigms and underscore the need for defenses that operate beyond the support of the clean distribution.

</details>


### [363] [ProRL: Effective Reinforcement Learning for Proactive Recommendation via Rectified Policy Gradient Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28293)
*Hongru Hou,Tiehua Mei,Denghui Geng,Jinhui Huang,Ao Xu,Hengrui Chen,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProRL框架，通过步进奖励中心化和位置特定优势估计两种机制，解决强化学习在主动推荐系统中梯度估计的偏差和方差问题，在三个真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推荐系统旨在引导用户偏好向目标物品偏移，强化学习可优化其序列决策任务。但直接应用策略梯度存在路径级奖励分解为步级奖励时的长度依赖偏差（使梯度偏向路径延伸而非有效探索）以及高梯度方差问题。

Method: 提出ProRL框架，包含两种机制：(1) 步进奖励中心化，通过减去期望奖励消除长度依赖偏差；(2) 位置特定优势估计，利用奖励分解结构计算步级依赖的基线以降低方差。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，ProRL显著优于当前最先进的主动推荐方法。

Conclusion: 所提机制使得策略梯度能够精确地以路径质量为目标，有效解决了传统策略梯度在主动推荐中的梯度估计缺陷。

Abstract: Proactive Recommender Systems (PRSs) aim to guide user preference shift toward target items by generating paths of intermediate recommendations. Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for optimizing such sequential decision tasks, as path rewards can naturally capture both short-term acceptance and long-term guidance effectiveness. However, naively applying policy gradients to PRS results in deficient gradient estimation. We identify two deficiencies: (1) path-level rewards decompose into step-level rewards with positive mean, creating a length-dependent bias that causes gradients to favor path extension over meaningful exploration; (2) weighting each step by the entire path-level reward ignores the decomposition structure, leading to high gradient variance. To rectify these two deficiencies, we propose an effective RL framework ProRL with two novel mechanisms for proactive recommendation. First, Stepwise Reward Centering subtracts expected rewards to neutralize length-dependent bias, ensuring that path extension yields zero expected gradient signal. Second, Position-Specific Advantage Estimation leverages the reward decomposition structure to compute step-dependent baselines, reducing gradient variance. Together, these mechanisms yield policy gradients that precisely target path quality. Our experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that ProRL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art PRSs. Our code is available at https://github.com/hongruhou89/ProRL.

</details>


### [364] [LEIA: Learned Environment for Interactive Architected Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28368)
*Haiqian Yang,Yuan Cao,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEIA世界模型，用于实时交互式模拟工程材料（特别是架构材料）在边界条件下的变形与应力场，并引入MicroPlate基准测试，证明其可加速新材料设计搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型可处理游戏环境和机器人操作，但真实工程材料具有非线性本构、历史依赖内状态、惯性动力学及多尺度层级结构，物理工程模拟仍超出其能力范围。

Method: LEIA采用自回归方式对用户指定的加载条件逐帧生成响应，处理大规模三维非结构化网格。提出MicroPlate基准，涵盖两种微结构建模模式（显式三维几何的架构晶格和隐式内自由度的均匀板），并对比四种基线方法。

Result: LEIA在MicroPlate基准上优于四种基线方法，并实现了基于应力的高效候选生成与排序，经有限元真实验证，可用于快速替代引导的架构材料从头设计搜索。

Conclusion: LEIA可作为工程材料交互式仿真的有效世界模型，显著加速架构材料的设计迭代过程。

Abstract: World models have enabled interactive exploration of game environments and robotic manipulation, but physical engineering remains beyond their reach: real materials exhibit nonlinear constitutive laws, carry history-dependent internal state, undergo inertial dynamics, and may possess hierarchical structures spanning multiple length scales. We present LEIA (Learned Environment for Interactive Architected materials), a world model that lets engineers apply boundary conditions step by step and observe the resulting deformation and stress fields in real time. LEIA handles large three-dimensional unstructured meshes and generates autoregressive responses to user-specified loading. We introduce MicroPlate, a benchmark of architected plates spanning two regimes of microstructure modeling: architected lattices that resolve microstructure explicitly through three-dimensional geometry, and a homogeneous plate where microstructural change is modeled implicitly through internal degrees of freedom. MicroPlate is used to assess LEIA alongside four baseline methods across both regimes. Finally, we demonstrate that LEIA enables efficient candidate generation and ranking for fast surrogate-guided search for de novo designs of architected materials, with stress-accurate candidate ranking validated by finite element ground truth.

</details>


### [365] [Stage-wise Distortion-Perception Traversal in Zero-shot Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28711)
*Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Leon Yan,Zhenyu Xiao,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP-RPS及其潜变量版本LMAP-RPS，通过分阶段框架（先MAP估计逼近MMSE提供低失真初始化，再重噪声后验采样逐步提升感知质量）在单个扩散模型上实现零样本逆问题的失真-感知权衡遍历，理论分析和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失真-感知（D-P）权衡是贝叶斯逆问题的基本现象，在实际应用中需要在推理时灵活遍历D-P权衡曲线，但现有扩散模型逆问题求解方法缺乏高效且 principled 的D-P遍历策略。

Method: 提出分阶段框架MAP-RPS：第一阶段进行MAP估计，近似MMSE解并提供低失真初始化；第二阶段进行重噪声后验采样，逐步提升感知质量。进一步将方法扩展到潜变量空间得到LMAP-RPS，利用大规模预训练潜扩散模型。对两个阶段分别给出理论分析。

Result: 大量实验表明MAP-RPS和LMAP-RPS在各种任务上实现了更有效的D-P权衡遍历，同时作为高效求解器在真实逆问题中表现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: 提出的分阶段框架能使用单个扩散模型在零样本逆问题中有效遍历D-P权衡，理论分析和实验共同验证了方法的有效性与广泛适用性。

Abstract: The distortion-perception (D-P) tradeoff is a fundamental phenomenon of Bayesian inverse problems, which characterizes the inherent tension between distortion performance and perceptual quality. Enabling flexible traversal of the D-P tradeoff at inference time is crucial for practical applications. Despite the recent success of diffusion models in zero-shot inverse problem solving, efficient and principled strategies for D-P traversal in diffusion-based inverse algorithms remain inadequately characterized. In this paper, we propose a stage-wise framework for realizing D-P traversal using a single diffusion model in zero-shot inverse problems. Our proposed method, termed MAP-RPS, starts with an MAP estimation stage that approximates the MMSE solution and provides a low-distortion initialization, followed by a re-noised posterior sampling stage that progressively improves perceptual quality. We provide theoretical analyses for both stages, establishing the validity and effectiveness of the proposed design. Furthermore, we extend MAP-RPS to the latent space, yielding LMAP-RPS, which enjoys broader applicability by leveraging large-scale pre-trained latent diffusion backbones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MAP-RPS and LMAP-RPS enable more effective D-P traversal on various tasks, while also exhibiting strong performance as efficient solvers for real-world inverse problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [366] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的actor-critic模型预测控制（AC-MPC）方法，通过加速可微MPC层的前向和反向传播，显著降低了训练和推理延迟，同时在无人机竞速任务中保持了最先进的圈速和近极限动态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AC-MPC方法中可微MPC层需要在前向和反向传播中反复求解优化问题，导致训练和推理延迟过高，成为实际部署的瓶颈。

Method: 提出CUDA加速的AC-MPC变体，通过GPU并行计算加速可微MPC层的优化求解过程，减少端到端执行时间。

Result: 在无人机竞速任务上，该方法实现了最先进的圈速和近极限动态行为，同时训练和推理时间显著减少。

Conclusion: CUDA加速有效解决了AC-MPC的计算瓶颈，在不牺牲控制性能的前提下大幅提升了效率。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [367] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出**动态对称性**（Dynamic Symmetry）概念，即机器人可达质心加速度的均匀性，并通过**动态各向同性**（Dynamic Isotropy）进行量化。在1000余种模拟形态中，高动态对称性一致提升了轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、抗干扰能力和能效。为逼近理论极限，开发了**Argus**系列球形机器人（径向线性致动器驱动），其中20足实物原型实现了近极端动态各向同性，展示了方向无关运动、杂乱/可变形地形灵活穿越、快速自稳定及部分致动器故障下的鲁棒性，并利用分布式感知实现全向感知与连续运动中的物体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在自然系统中是核心组织原则，但在机器人领域，对称性设计主要局限于几何形态，缺乏在动力学层面的统一设计策略。本文旨在探索**将对称性从形态层面提升至动态致动能力层面**，研究其对机器人性能的系统性影响。

Method: 1. 提出**动态对称性**概念及**动态各向同性**量化指标；2. 对1000余种模拟形态进行系统性实验，评估动态对称性对多种性能指标的影响；3. 开发**Argus**系列球形机器人（以径向线性致动器为核心架构），通过改变致动几何构型实现不同动态对称性水平；4. 构建20足实物原型，验证近极限动态各向同性下的多维度实际表现。

Result: 模拟实验：高动态对称性在轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、抗干扰性和能效上均表现更优，且益处随动态各向同性逼近理论极限而愈发显著。实物实验（20足Argus原型）：实现了近极端动态各向同性，具备方向无关运动、复杂/可变形地形灵活穿越、快速自稳定、部分致动器故障下的恢复能力，以及分布式感知支持的全向感知与运动中物体交互。

Conclusion: 在机器人设计中，不仅要在形态上追求对称，更要**在可达动力学层面追求对称**，这是一种实现敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性的通用且有效的设计范式，尤其适用于不确定的陆地与外星环境。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [368] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo框架利用单臂机器人演示与人类协作数据，通过数据增强生成双臂机器人合成演示，实现双臂操纵策略的学习，在五项任务中最高成功率达70%，且少量微调可进一步提升65-70%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协调操纵对现实任务至关重要，但双臂机器人和数据集的稀缺限制了策略学习；而单臂机器人广泛可用，因此研究如何利用单臂机器人数据训练双臂策略。

Method: 通过单臂机器人遥操作（人类协作完成另一侧任务并交换角色）收集数据，利用手部姿态估计、图像/点云分割和修复技术，将RGB-D观测数据增强为面向目标双臂机器人的合成演示，基于此训练双臂策略。

Result: 在箱体搬运、背包打包、衣物折叠、外套拉链和盘子交接五项任务中，零样本部署成功率最高达70%；仅需25个目标机器人演示进行少样本微调，成功率相比从头训练提升65-70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo有效实现了从单臂机器人数据到双臂机器人策略的知识迁移，解决了双臂数据稀缺问题，为大规模双臂策略学习提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [369] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 是运动规划库的重大升级版本，引入硬件加速支持实时规划，并与现代AI研究流程无缝集成。该库自2008年发布以来，已成为采样式运动规划算法的基准实现平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足实时运动规划需求，通过硬件加速提升规划效率，并更好地支持与AI研究工作的集成。

Method: 在原有采样式规划算法基础上，新增硬件加速能力，扩展了渐近最优规划器、惰性规划器、约束运动规划和时序逻辑目标规划等功能模块。

Result: 发布了OMPL 2.0，实现了对实时运动规划的支持，并与现代AI研究流程实现无缝集成。

Conclusion: OMPL与运动规划领域在过去近二十年间共同成长，该库对研究社区产生了广泛而深远的影响。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [370] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相位条件化、力感知的框架用于鲁棒的变形物体操作。通过FiLM条件化的ACT编码器、多模态相位预测器（融合视觉、力、位姿）和混合阻抗控制器，实现闭环分层架构。在双臂挂取T恤任务中，挂放成功率从56%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时依赖马尔可夫假设，导致视觉相似但需要矛盾动作的情况下出现状态混叠，且无法从执行失败中自主恢复。

Method: 1) FiLM条件化的ACT编码器，根据当前任务相位调制特征提取；2) 融合视觉、力、位姿的多模态相位预测器，实时估计相位并检测接触失败；3) 混合阻抗控制器用于柔顺执行；4) 力感知遥操作界面用于数据采集。

Result: FiLM调制显著优于无条件和token级条件基线；t-SNE分析显示FiLM产生相位特异性特征表示；双臂T恤挂放任务成功率从56%提升至87%。

Conclusion: 闭环分层架构结合相位条件化和力感知，能有效解决变形物体操作中的状态混叠和错误恢复问题，提升操作鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [371] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双无人机空中对接平台，采用主从编队和被动磁锁紧模块，实现了轻小型四旋翼在推力与载荷约束下的可靠空中对接，并提供了完整的软硬件评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机之间的可靠空中对接对于模块化空中协作与操作至关重要，但现有方案在推力、载荷约束下难以实现精确的相对位姿控制和可重复的对接。

Method: 采用双四旋翼主从编队，设计轻量化模块化框架与被动磁锁紧机构；提出进度感知的任务管理器管理四个阶段（接近、对准、捕获、稳定）；集成ROS 2与Crazyflie/PX4接口，实现同步日志记录。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，使用编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接时间及失败模式统计等指标进行定量评估。

Conclusion: 该平台支持对接监督与同步策略的统计比较，为模块化空中协作和可重复空中对接提供了实用的测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints.
