We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [372] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出3DVLA，一种即插即用的框架，通过显式多视图一致性约束、实例估计模块和掩码自监督3D编码，为预训练VLA注入鲁棒的3D推理能力，显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解，表现为三个相互关联的挑战：弱3D空间位置提取（缺乏多视图一致性）、不足的3D实例理解、以及遮挡下的脆弱推理。虽然已有成熟的3D感知方法，但直接集成到VLA中面临架构不兼容和昂贵的实例级标注依赖问题。

Method: 3DVLA包含三个核心模块：(1) 跨模态的显式多视图一致性约束3D特征编码，结合空间条件几何聚合方法；(2) 使用高层级实例令牌的实例估计模块，实现3D实例感知；(3) 掩码自监督3D编码分支，保留预测器进行视觉令牌补全以处理遮挡。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0基准上，集成多个VLA基线后，操作性能取得持续且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 3DVLA作为即插即用框架，无需额外人工标注或丢弃VLM先验，即可有效为预训练VLA注入3D推理能力，验证了其有效性和兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [373] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA提出一种即插即用的阶段自适应推理框架，通过动态计算调度在视觉编码器、大语言模型和动作头之间联合分配计算资源，无需修改或重训练基础模型，在GR00T和CogACT上实现最高3.77倍加速，并将真实机器人控制频率从13.8 Hz提升至26.3 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率受限，难以满足实时机器人操作需求。现有加速方法多优化单一组件或依赖固定加速规则，忽略了序列化具身控制中不同控制步骤推理需求的非均匀性。受人类运动控制中认知与反馈资源集中在目标敏感阶段的启发，作者认为VLA模型应学会何时投入完整计算、何时复用先前计算。

Method: 提出ElegantVLA即插即用的阶段自适应推理框架，包含：1) 轻量级调度器，通过观察时序表示相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进度，联合分配视觉编码器、LLM和动作头的计算资源；2) 感知-语言推理阶段，根据视觉-语言表示稳定性选择五级Vision-LLM计算模式（从完全重计算到多步时序复用）；3) 动作生成阶段，选择三级去噪模式（稳定运动时复用中间去噪状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整精炼）。

Result: 在GR00T和CogACT上分别实现2.55倍和3.77倍加速；在6个真实世界GR00T任务中，计算量减少2.18倍，控制频率从13.8 Hz提升至26.3 Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA作为一种通用的加速框架，适用于现代具有显式动作生成模块的VLA管线，无需修改或重训练基础模型，能有效平衡计算效率与任务性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [374] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人机交互的强化学习框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏方法，将视觉教师策略的知识蒸馏到仅依赖位姿、扭转和力感知的无视觉学生策略中，实现快速训练和强任务泛化能力。在NIST装配基准板上，经过约50分钟训练，3个代表性任务达到95%成功率，并能泛化到8个未见任务变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉增强的强化学习策略容易过拟合训练时的视觉条件，限制了其鲁棒性和可迁移性。现有方法依赖域随机化或数据增强，但本文希望在真实世界中训练，无需这些技术，同时实现鲁棒的多任务变体泛化。

Method: 提出人机交互的强化学习框架，采用教师-学生蒸馏范式：先训练一个视觉增强的教师策略，然后将其知识蒸馏到一个仅依赖位姿、扭转和力/力矩感知的无视觉学生策略中。整个训练在真实世界中进行，无需域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在真实NIST装配基准板上，3个代表性任务经过约50分钟训练达到95%总体成功率，并能泛化到8个未见任务变体。在最具挑战性的任务上，通过蒸馏微调实现完全成功。策略在鲁棒性和适应性上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够结合视觉策略的快速训练优势和无视觉策略的强泛化能力，在真实世界接触丰富的机器人操作任务中实现高效、鲁棒且可迁移的强化学习。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [375] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解释的计算框架，利用多感官触觉数据对材料感知进行建模。该框架包含三个模型：将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感知属性、基于感知表征进行材料分类、以及直接从触觉特征分类材料。研究发现，结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互能提高预测准确性，热感线索对感知建模和材料分类尤为重要。这些发现强调了触觉和热感线索的重要性，可为未来机器人手指和触觉显示器的设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对材料的触觉感知依赖复杂的多感官触觉线索，但低层触觉信号与感知表征之间的关系尚未明晰，这阻碍了触觉在数字环境中的整合以及具有类人触觉感知能力的机器人发展。

Method: 提出了一个可解释的计算框架，包含三个相互关联的模型：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感知属性；模型2基于感知表征进行材料分类；模型3直接从触觉特征分类材料。利用按压、静态接触和滑动交互的多模态触觉数据进行分析。

Result: 结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互的信息能提高预测准确性；热感线索对感知建模和材料分类尤为有效；触觉和热感线索在当前的机器人手指和触觉显示器中代表性不足。

Conclusion: 热感和触觉线索对于提升人工系统近似人类材料感知能力至关重要，将这些线索整合到机器人手指和触觉界面中，有助于设计更具感知基础的人机交互界面。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [376] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-Seg框架通过风格扩展和纹理正则化，提升越野环境下语义分割对分布偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境下，源-目标域差异和传感器损坏导致分布偏移，使语义分割模型预测不准确，进而导致导航任务失败。

Method: 提出ST-Seg框架，包含风格扩展（SE）生成多样化真实风格以扩充源域风格信息，以及纹理正则化（TR）通过深度纹理流形稳定风格增强学习中的局部纹理表征。

Result: 在多种分布偏移的目标域上，ST-Seg显著优于现有方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ST-Seg增强了语义分割在越野导航中的实际应用鲁棒性，为处理分布偏移提供了直观有效的方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [377] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLAConf，一种基于单类判别性置信度框架，利用冻结的预训练VLA内部表示和轻量级置信度头，在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数，避免了穷举重采样的开销，显著提升了推理效率，并在LIBERO基准和真实机器人实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型置信度估计方法（基于集成范式或动作token概率）存在计算效率低和跨架构泛化性差的问题，需要重复采样且仅适用于离散动作空间，难以应用于连续动作空间。

Method: 提出VLAConf框架：利用冻结的预训练VLA内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数；同时使用步骤条件建模编码操作轨迹中的 rollout 阶段信息。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，VLAConf显著提升了后验校准中构建的置信度信号质量，在推理效率上大幅超越现有基线；真实机器人实验进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf通过单类判别性置信度框架解决了现有方法在计算效率和跨架构泛化性上的局限，为VLA模型在开放世界机器人操作任务中提供了高效、通用的置信度估计方案。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [378] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhAIL基准测试框架，采用时间-成功累积分布函数(CDF)作为评估原语，通过人类相对吞吐量(HRT)评分和Kolmogorov-Smirnov显著性检验，在N≤30次 rollout 的小样本条件下实现了比传统二值成功率更可靠的VLA策略比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略评估依赖固定超时下的二值成功率，样本量小(N≤25)且缺乏置信区间和统计比较，难以可靠区分性能接近的策略。

Method: 提出PhAIL基准：1) 以时间-成功CDF作为评估基元；2) 采用人类相对吞吐量(HRT)标量评分，附Bootstrap置信区间；3) 使用Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验进行逐对象和宏平均的显著性比较。

Result: 在4个公开VLA上，宏平均KS检验在N≤30条件下解决了GR00T vs. ACT和OpenPI vs. ACT两个接近比较，而二值指标无法区分；OpenPI vs. GR00T仍无法解决。最佳VLA比人类参考慢约7倍。

Conclusion: 基于分布（CDF）的评估方法相比传统二值成功率在小样本下更敏感可靠，推荐作为VLA策略的标准评估实践。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [379] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARS策略通过自适应地在需要时注入随机性（多模态生成）而在单模态阶段回归确定性学习，在保持多模态表达能力的同时显著提升训练和推理效率，在模拟和真实任务中分别实现16.67%的成功率提升和83.20%的推理延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习中的生成式策略（如扩散模型）依赖随机噪声初始化和迭代去噪过程来实现多模态行为，导致训练复杂度高、推理效率低；然而并非机器人任务的所有阶段都需要行为多样性，因此有必要自适应地选择何时启用多模态生成。

Method: 提出Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS)策略，在需要行为多样性的阶段激活多模态生成（注入适量噪声），在单模态阶段回退到高效的确定性学习，实现噪声注入的时机与强度自适应控制。

Result: 在8个模拟任务和4个真实世界任务上，MARS保持鲁棒的多模态表达能力，真实测试中成功率提升16.67%，推理延迟降低83.20%；在近确定性任务上，MARS的训练效率甚至超过确定性策略。

Conclusion: MARS弥合了生成式策略的多模态能力与确定性模型的高效性之间的鸿沟，验证了"只在恰当时间注入恰当噪声"这一思路在机器人模仿学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [380] [Joint Angle Estimation with Customized Wristband Based on Online Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29771)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaobin Chen,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线增量学习的智能腕带系统，用于估计腕关节角度。该方法采用两阶段策略：第一阶段利用IMU数据作为地面真值进行在线学习，第二阶段仅用腕带传感器完成角度估计。实验表明在不同测试配置下（左右手腕、佩戴位置偏差、不同受试者）误差约为15度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感研究大多方法简单、难以适应不同场景，缺乏对数据漂移（如佩戴位置变化、不同受试者差异等）的自适应能力，因此需要一种能在线自适应、实用且舒适的腕关节角度估计方法。

Method: 提出两阶段在线增量学习方法：第一阶段利用IMU作为地面真值，通过在线学习更新模型以适应佩戴者手腕运动特征；第二阶段仅使用腕带传感器进行腕关节角度估计。模型训练在数据采集过程中完成。

Result: 传感器在不同应变变化下表现良好，系统在不同场景下腕关节轨迹估计误差约为15度。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效适应数据漂移问题（包括左右手腕差异、同一手腕佩戴位置偏差、不同受试者差异），兼具舒适性和实用性，为智能可穿戴在运动健康监测中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Intelligent wearable technology plays an increasingly important role in human-computer interaction, motion, and health monitoring. To ensure comfort and practicality of use, one common form for motion monitoring is to utilize soft wearable sensors. However, many research applications regarding wearable sensors are simplistic and difficult to adapt to different situations. This study proposes a system for estimating the angle of the wrist joint using a customized wristband based on an online incremental learning approach. It is a two-stage estimation method: the first stage updates the model based on the wearer's wrist movement characteristics using online learning, integrating real-time data from an IMU as ground truth. The second stage utilizes the updated model for estimation of wrist joint angle solely with the wristband. In other words, model training is completed during data acquisition, allowing the trained model to be used for subsequent angle estimation. This method offers advantages in adapting to data drift caused by variations in different testing configurations, such as the left and right wrists of the same subject, deviations in the wearing position on the same wrist, and even differences among various subjects. The results indicate that the sensors exhibit good performance under strain variations, and the wrist joint trajectory estimation of the proposed system has an approximate error of 15 degree in different scenarios.

</details>


### [381] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出未来体验调节（FEC）框架，通过将闭环策略条件化在短时未来视频的潜在表征上，为多步机器人操作提供结构化先验。在模拟环境中，未来片段通过LLM推理器、数字孪生滚动和视频扩散模型三阶段生成。在RoboCasa和CALVIN上的实验表明，生成式未来优于无未来条件，而错误未来会降低性能；BC+RL实例化取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步机器人操作需要在场景演化不确定性下进行探索和策略适应，现有方法缺乏有效的结构化先验来指导这一过程。

Method: 提出FEC框架，将闭环策略条件化在短时未来视频的潜在表征上。未来片段分三阶段生成：1）LLM推理器基于任务本体和当前场景状态推理；2）无机器人数字孪生滚动模拟目标物体运动；3）无掩码视频扩散模型合成与机器人一致的未来片段。主要用BC和BC+RL实例化，并与基于未来条件的Streaming Flow Policy (SFP)基线对比。

Result: 在RoboCasa和CALVIN上，生成式未来（GenFuture）优于无未来条件（NoFuture），错误未来（WrongFuture）降低性能。BC+RL实例化取得最佳结果。CALVIN的8任务学习曲线分析显示：GTFuture学习最快，GenFuture比NoFuture学习更早且达到更高水平，WrongFuture全程为零。

Conclusion: 短时未来视频可作为探索和策略适应的有效结构化先验，即使在存在不完美未来预测的情况下也能发挥作用。

Abstract: Multi-step robot manipulation requires acting under uncertainty about how the scene will evolve, making exploration and policy adaptation challenging. We study whether short-horizon, task-consistent future videos can provide useful structured priors for control and reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. We formalize this idea through Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), a simple interface that conditions closed-loop policies on a latent representation of a short future video. In our simulation setup, future clips are generated in three stages, an LLM reasoner operating over a task ontology initialized from the current scene state, a robot-free digital-twin rollout of the intended object motion, and a mask-free video diffusion model that synthesizes a robot-consistent future clip without requiring segmentation at inference. We instantiate this future-conditioning interface primarily with BC and BC+RL, and compare against a future-conditioned Streaming Flow Policy (SFP) baseline on RoboCasa and CALVIN under NoFuture, GTFuture, GenFuture, and WrongFuture. Generated futures improve performance over no-future conditioning, while mismatched futures degrade it, and our BC+RL instantiation achieves the strongest overall results. An average BC+RL learning-curve analysis across 8 CALVIN tasks further shows that GTFuture improves fastest, GenFuture improves earlier and to a higher level than NoFuture, and WrongFuture remains at zero throughout training. These results suggest that short-horizon future videos can serve as useful structured priors for exploration and policy adaptation under imperfect future predictions. https://enact2026.github.io/

</details>


### [382] [Fisher-Preserving Guidance: Training-Free Manifold Constraints for Safe Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29937)
*Hao Ren,Zetong Bi,Yiming Zeng,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Zhaoliang Wan,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过低秩Jacobian分解实现Fisher保持更新，并引入Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity作为不确定性信号用于鲁棒的多样本动作融合，在多种导航和操控任务上优于扩散策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型的采样和测试时引导产生的轨迹不可靠或低效，因为更新会偏离训练流形，导致Fisher漂移。

Method: 1) 通过低秩Jacobian分解计算Fisher保持更新，每步仅需一次反向传播；2) 提出Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity作为不确定性信号；3) 用于鲁棒的多样本动作融合（multi-sample action blending）。

Result: 在Maze2D、PushT以及仿真和真实机器人视觉导航任务上，均一致优于强扩散策略基线。

Conclusion: 该方法无需额外训练即可提升扩散模型在导航和操控任务中的轨迹可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models are effective for waypoint prediction in visual navigation, but standard sampling and test time guidance can produce unreliable or inefficient trajectories when updates drift off the training manifold. We propose Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection, a training-free inference method that avoids large Fisher drift associated with off-distribution actions while optimizing a task objective. Our method computes the Fisher-preserving update via a low-rank Jacobian factorization, requiring only a single backward pass per step and enabling real-time use. We further introduce Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity as an uncertainty signal and use it for robust multi-sample action blending. Experiments on toy and realistic navigation benchmarks, including Maze2D with TSDF-based guidance, PushT with official Diffusion Policy weights, and visual navigation in simulation and on real robots, demonstrate consistent improvements in performance over strong diffusion-policy baselines without additional training.

</details>


### [383] [Replicable Simulation-Based Robot Validation through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973)
*Argentina Ortega,Samuel Wiest,Frederik Pasch,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在基于仿真的机器人测试中集成数据溯源与FAIR原则，通过追踪工件关联和附加机器可读元数据来提升测试的可复现性，并在移动机器人导航数据集上进行了验证，同时讨论了集成过程中的障碍与建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真测试的可复现性严重依赖测试配置、执行和后处理过程的透明文档记录，而现有方法往往缺乏对数据来源和设计决策的系统性追踪，导致证据链难以端到端重建。

Method: 在现有仿真测试框架中增强溯源追踪与元数据收集机制，将溯源和元数据集成到测试流程中而非事后添加，并用结构化溯源和FAIR元数据丰富移动机器人导航数据集。

Result: 成功在仿真测试框架中实现了溯源追踪与元数据收集的集成，生成了带有结构化溯源和FAIR对齐元数据的移动机器人导航数据集。

Conclusion: 数据溯源与FAIR原则必须嵌入测试流程本身而非事后处理；集成过程中面临词汇对齐、属性选择、领域标准采纳等障碍，并给出了可操作的建议。

Abstract: Robot behavior is often validated through simulation-based testing, yet the replicability of such campaigns depends critically on transparent documentation of how tests are configured, executed, and post-processed. We argue that data provenance, coupled with the FAIR principles (findability, accessibility, interoperability, and reusability), addresses this gap by explicitly tracking links between artifacts and by attaching machine-readable metadata about file origins and key design decisions. Moreover, provenance and metadata cannot be treated as an afterthought confined to final datasets; they must be integrated into the testing processes that generate those datasets so that evidence can be reconstructed end-to-end. We demonstrate this by augmenting an existing simulation-based testing framework with provenance tracking and metadata collection mechanisms, and by using these extensions to enrich a mobile robot navigation dataset with structured provenance and FAIR-aligned metadata. Finally, we discuss obstacles encountered in this integration -- such as vocabulary alignment, attribute selection, and adoption of domain standards -- and provide actionable recommendations for implementing provenance-centric, FAIR metadata in robotics validation workflows.

</details>


### [384] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yifan Han,Yanming Shao,Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BORA是一个面向真实世界灵巧操作的离线到在线RL后训练框架，通过引入动作条件价值引导的评论家和轻量级人机循环的块状残差适应机制，解决了VLA模型在灵巧操作中的高维控制和时间不一致性问题，在五个复杂灵巧任务中平均成功率提升33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实世界灵巧操作中面临高维手部控制和累积执行误差两大挑战，使得真实世界RL后训练成为必要，但高维灵巧探索又引发时间不一致性、样本低效和硬件风险等问题。

Method: BORA采用两阶段框架：离线阶段构建一个同时接收VLM认知token和动作块的评论家，实现动作条件价值引导；在线阶段冻结VLA基座，引入轻量级人机循环(Human-in-the-Loop)块状残差适应机制，结合离线评论家和干预驱动奖励来纠正执行偏差。

Result: 在五个复杂真实世界灵巧任务上，BORA相比纯模仿学习和传统解耦RL基线，标准设置下平均成功率绝对提升33%，未见物体泛化场景下提升43%。

Conclusion: BORA通过离线到在线的RL后训练框架有效弥合了视觉引导动作生成与物理可靠灵巧执行之间的差距，同时保留了预训练策略作为稳定先验，实现了对真实世界物理差异的鲁棒适应。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>


### [385] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Jinhui Ye,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Jie Zhang,Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Pei Lin,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Shuai Bai,Jingren Zhou,Jiazhao Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Ye Wang,Yiyang Huang,Zixing Lei,Wujian Peng,Delin Chen,Yingming Zheng,Jingyang Fan,Xianwei Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Haoyang Li,Anzhe Chen,Tong Zhang,Xuejing Liu,Yuchong Sun,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Chenxu Lü,Zhibo Yang,Tao Yu,Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA是一个统一的具身基础模型，通过DiT动作解码器将视觉-语言模型扩展到连续动作生成，支持操作、导航和轨迹预测等异构任务，在多个基准测试上取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能研究通常为单个任务（如操作或导航）设计专用模型，导致能力碎片化且跨任务、跨环境、跨机器人本体的泛化能力有限。本文旨在探索是否可以将异构的具身决策问题统一到一个视觉-语言-动作模型中。

Method: 基于Qwen的视觉-语言模型，通过DiT（Diffusion Transformer）动作解码器扩展至连续动作生成；采用大规模联合预训练策略，融合机器人操作轨迹、人类示教数据、合成仿真数据、视觉-语言导航数据、轨迹监督及辅助视觉-语言数据；引入具身感知提示条件化（embodiment-aware prompt conditioning），使用机器人特定文本描述指定本体和控制约定；将操作、导航和轨迹预测统一为动作-轨迹预测框架。

Result: LIBERO 97.9%，Simpler-WidowX 73.7%，RoboTwin-Easy/Hard 86.1%/87.2%，R2R OSR 69.0%，RxR SR 59.6%，真实ALOHA实验平均OOD成功率76.9%，DOMINO动态操作零样本成功率26.6%。

Conclusion: Qwen-VLA在操作、导航和轨迹中心基准测试上展现了一致的多任务性能和跨场景布局、背景、光照、物体配置及机器人本体的分布外泛化能力，验证了将异构具身决策问题统一到单一VLA模型中的可行性。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


### [386] [Gaze2Act: Gaze-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Interactive Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30282)
*Kuangji Zuo,Gen Li,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Boyu Ma,Shijia Han,Xinyu Zhou,Xichen Yuan,Chuhao Zhou,Jiaqi Bai,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gaze2Act框架，利用人类注视作为动态直观的意图信号，通过跨视角语义匹配将第一人称注视映射到机器人视角，以粗到细的方式指定目标，在Unitree G1人形机器人上16项真实任务中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯语言指令难以精确传达人类意图，尤其是在区分相似物体、指定交互位置以及应对执行过程中的目标变化时存在明显不足。

Method: 1) 跨视角语义匹配桥接第一人称与机器人视角的注视映射；2) 粗到细的目标指定（物体掩码+注视点）；3) 感知层提示与动作层条件化将注视信号集成到策略中。

Result: 在7类任务16项真实机器人任务中，Gaze2Act在意图准确率和任务成功率上均达到SOTA，在物体消歧、细粒度交互和动态意图引导方面显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 人类注视是一种自然、低负担、高表达力的模态，非常适合人机协同的VLA控制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown strong potential for robot learning by following language instructions. However, in practice, language alone is often insufficient to precisely convey human intent. It is difficult to describe which exact object to interact with among similar candidates, where to act on the object, or how the target may change during execution. To address this limitation, we propose Gaze2Act, a novel VLA framework that leverages human gaze as a dynamic and intuitive intent signal for complex interactive manipulation. Gaze2Act first bridges the ego-exo view gap by mapping first-person gaze into the robot's perspective through cross-view semantic matching, producing both an object mask and a gaze point for coarse-to-fine target specification. These cues are then integrated into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning, allowing the robot to attend to relevant regions and execute precise interactions under dynamic intent. In a systematic evaluation across seven task categories and 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate. It notably outperforms baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering. These results demonstrate that human gaze provides a natural, low-burden, and highly expressive modality for human-in-the-loop VLA control.

</details>


### [387] [A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30313)
*Yufei Jia,Zhanxiang Cao,Mingrui Yu,Heng Zhang,Shenyu Chen,Dixuan Jiang,Meng Li,Xiaofan Li,Yiyang Liu,Junzhe Wu,Zheng Li,XiLin Fang,Tingyu Cui,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Anqi Wang,Zifan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Chenyu Cao,Zhenbiao Huang,Ziang Zheng,Jie Lu,Xin Ma,Zhengyang Wei,Xiang Zhao,Tianyue Zhan,Ye He,Yuxiang Chen,Yizhou Jiang,Yue Li,Haizhou Ge,Yuhang Dong,Fan Jia,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiwa Deng,Zhixing Chen,Hanyang Shao,Chenxin Dong,Yixuan Li,Yizhi Chen,Bokui Chen,Kaifeng Zhang,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Xiang Li,Yue Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLab 是一种异构 CPU-仿真 / GPU-学习 架构，通过统一运行时解耦 CPU 并行仿真与 GPU 策略更新，在相同硬件配置下将端到端训练效率提升 3-10 倍，减少对 NVIDIA CUDA 软件栈的依赖，支持多平台（macOS、AMD ROCm、Intel XPU）执行，证明 GPU 仿真并非高效训练的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于仿真的机器人强化学习默认假设高效训练要求物理引擎必须在 GPU 上运行，作者质疑这一假设，认为核心问题在于仿真吞吐量、策略学习与运行时同步是否形成高效的端到端循环，而非物理引擎运行的处理器。

Method: 提出 UniLab 异构架构，采用 CPU 批处理仿真后端（MuJoCoUni 和 MotrixSim）配合 GPU 策略学习，通过统一运行时管理数据移动、缓冲和同步，解耦仿真与学习流程，支持 PPO、SAC、FlashSAC、TD3、APPO 等多种算法。

Result: 在代表性机器人控制任务上，UniLab 在相同硬件配置下实现 3-10 倍的端到端训练效率提升，减少对 NVIDIA CUDA 软件栈的依赖，并支持 Apple macOS、AMD ROCm 和 Intel XPU 等跨平台执行。

Conclusion: GPU 仿真虽是一条有效的训练加速路径，但并非必要路径，UniLab 拓宽了机器人强化学习训练的实用系统选择。

Abstract: Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.

</details>


### [388] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Fenghao Yu,Zhehuan Chen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yejin Choi,David Held,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWits是一个双手机器人基准测试，通过自动化任务生成管道（多智能体协作框架）创建了30个种子任务和208个变异任务，用于系统评估机器人的认知推理、创造性工具使用和对意外条件的鲁棒性。实验发现预训练VLA在种子任务微调后表现尚可，但在变异任务中表现脆弱，缺乏推理和策略适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试主要强调技能级执行，缺乏对认知推理能力（如推理、适应和创造性问题解决）的系统评估，因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出RoboWits双手机器人基准测试，并设计了一个自动化任务生成管道，该管道采用多智能体协作框架，包含种子任务生成与验证、指标生成、场景生成和任务变异四个智能体，通过几何、材料和装配推理三个维度对任务进行难度分级和变异。

Result: 预训练VLA在种子任务上经单任务微调后取得初步成功，但在变异任务上表现不佳，暴露出在需要推理、策略适应和在欺骗性或受限环境中保持鲁棒性的操作任务中的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 当前机器人策略在认知推理和意外条件鲁棒性方面存在显著性能差距，RoboWits基准有效揭示了现有VLA在推理和策略适应方面的局限性。

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.

</details>


### [389] [Phantom: Training Robots Without Robots Using Only Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00779)
*Marion Lepert,Jiaying Fang,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需机器人数据、仅从人类视频演示中训练机器人操作策略的可扩展框架，通过手部姿态估计和视觉数据编辑将人类演示转化为机器人兼容的观察-动作对，实现零样本部署，在多种任务上取得高达92%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练通用机器人严重依赖遥操作演示，难以扩展；而人类视频数据丰富易得，但缺乏直接用于机器人训练的范式。作者希望利用大规模人类视频数据来训练机器人策略，降低对昂贵机器人数据的依赖。

Method: 通过手部姿态估计将人类演示中的手部动作映射为机器人动作，同时采用图像修复技术去除人类手臂，并叠加渲染的机器人模型以对齐视觉域，生成机器人兼容的观察-动作对，无需任何微调即可零样本部署到真实机器人。

Result: 在可变形物体操作、多物体清扫、插入等多种任务上取得高达92%的成功率，且能泛化到新环境并支持闭环执行。

Conclusion: 仅使用人类视频即可训练出有效的机器人操作策略，为可扩展的机器人学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Training general-purpose robots requires learning from large and diverse data sources. Current approaches rely heavily on teleoperated demonstrations which are difficult to scale. We present a scalable framework for training manipulation policies directly from human video demonstrations, requiring no robot data. Our method converts human demonstrations into robot-compatible observation-action pairs using hand pose estimation and visual data editing. We inpaint the human arm and overlay a rendered robot to align the visual domains. This enables zero-shot deployment on real hardware without any fine-tuning. We demonstrate strong success rates-up to 92%-on a range of tasks including deformable object manipulation, multi-object sweeping, and insertion. Our approach generalizes to novel environments and supports closed-loop execution. By demonstrating that effective policies can be trained using only human videos, our method broadens the path to scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [390] [Practical Insights on Grasp Strategies for Mobile Manipulation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12512)
*Isabella Huang,Richard Cheng,Sangwoon Kim,Dan Kruse,Carolyn Chen,Lukas Kaul,JC Hancock,Shanmuga Harikumar,Mark Tjersland,James Borders,Dan Helmick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SHOPPER，一个面向真实杂货店场景的移动操作机器人平台，旨在提高非结构化环境中抓取策略的可靠性和泛化性，并通过数百次实际抓取测试分析了关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的移动操作机器人在非结构化环境中可靠抓取物品的能力仍存在显著差距，阻碍了其大规模实际部署。

Method: 开发了SHOPPER移动操作机器人平台，在真实杂货店这一极具挑战性的环境中设计和部署通用抓取策略，并进行数百次实际抓取测试与详细分析。

Result: 通过数百次不同抓取尝试，识别了关键的失败模式，获得了一系列实际部署中的定量与定性发现。

Conclusion: 为机器人社区提供了有价值的实践见解，揭示了抓取领域亟待解决的开放性问题。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation robots are continuously advancing, with their grasping capabilities rapidly progressing. However, there are still significant gaps preventing state-of-the-art mobile manipulators from widespread real-world deployments, including their ability to reliably grasp items in unstructured environments. To help bridge this gap, we developed SHOPPER, a mobile manipulation robot platform designed to push the boundaries of reliable and generalizable grasp strategies. We develop these grasp strategies and deploy them in a real-world grocery store -- an exceptionally challenging setting chosen for its vast diversity of manipulable items, fixtures, and layouts. In this work, we present our detailed approach to designing general grasp strategies towards picking any item in a real grocery store. Additionally, we provide an in-depth analysis of our latest real-world field test, discussing key findings related to fundamental failure modes over hundreds of distinct pick attempts. Through our detailed analysis, we aim to offer valuable practical insights and identify key grasping challenges, which can guide the robotics community towards pressing open problems in the field.

</details>


### [391] [Follow Everything: A Leader-Following and Obstacle Avoidance Framework with Goal-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19399)
*Qianyi Zhang,Shijian Ma,Boyi Liu,Jianhao Jiao,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的机器人领导者跟随框架，使用分割模型替代检测模型以支持任意形态的领导者，通过距离帧缓冲区增强识别鲁棒性，并设计目标感知自适应机制与基于图的规划器，实现高效、避障的跟随，实验验证了其在多种场景下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人领导者跟随方法难以泛化到任意形态的领导者，且在领导者暂时离开视野时常常失效，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 1) 用分割模型替代检测模型，支持任意形态领导者；2) 引入距离帧缓冲区存储多距离下的领导者嵌入以增强识别鲁棒性；3) 设计目标感知自适应机制根据领导者可见性与运动控制机器人规划状态；4) 使用基于图的规划器为各状态生成候选轨迹，实现跟随与避障。

Result: 在四足机器人跟随多种领导者（人、地面机器人、无人机、四足机器人、停车标志）的仿真与真实实验中，在跟随成功率、视觉丢失时长、碰撞率和跟随距离等指标上均取得改进。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效解决了任意形态领导者跟随及视觉丢失时的鲁棒性问题，提升了机器人领导者跟随的泛化能力和可靠性。

Abstract: Robust and flexible leader-following is a critical capability for robots to integrate into human society. While existing methods struggle to generalize to leaders of arbitrary form and often fail when the leader temporarily leaves the robot's field of view, this work introduces a unified framework addressing both challenges. First, traditional detection models are replaced with a segmentation model, allowing the leader to be anything. To enhance recognition robustness, a distance frame buffer is implemented that stores leader embeddings at multiple distances, accounting for the unique characteristics of leader-following tasks. Second, a goal-aware adaptation mechanism is designed to govern robot planning states based on the leader's visibility and motion, complemented by a graph-based planner that generates candidate trajectories for each state, ensuring efficient following with obstacle avoidance. Simulations and real-world experiments with a legged robot follower and various leaders (human, ground robot, UAV, legged robot, stop sign) in both indoor and outdoor environments show competitive improvements in follow success rate, reduced visual loss duration, lower collision rate, and decreased leader-follower distance.

</details>


### [392] [Simulation-based planning of Motion Sequences for Automated Procedure Optimization in Multi-Robot Assembly Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23270)
*Loris Schneider,Marc Ungen,Elias Huber,Jan-Felix Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于仿真的方法，用于生成可重构多机器人装配单元中优化、协调的无碰撞运动序列，通过将装配步骤分解为核心操作与遍历操作，并采用基于分解的运动规划策略，在调度优化中集成可行的遍历操作，最终生成了优于分散式基线方法的装配流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构多机器人单元能够满足波动的装配需求，但其配置的循环规划带来了新挑战，特别是在生成优化的、协调的多机器人运动序列以最小化装配时间方面。

Method: 将装配步骤分解为任务相关的核心操作和连接性的遍历操作；核心操作受约束且预定义，遍历操作具有优化潜力；将核心操作的调度建模为优化问题，采用基于分解的运动规划策略集成可行的遍历操作；探索了采样启发式、树搜索和无梯度优化等多种求解技术；针对运动规划，提出了一种分解方法，识别调度中可独立求解的特定区域，并使用修改后的集中式路径规划算法。

Result: 该方法生成了高效且无碰撞的多机器人装配流程，优于依赖分散式、机器人个体运动规划的基线方法，并通过仿真实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于仿真的方法能够有效解决可重构多机器人装配单元中的协调运动序列优化问题，为应对波动装配需求提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reconfigurable multi-robot cells offer a promising approach to meet fluctuating assembly demands. However, the recurrent planning of their configurations introduces new challenges, particularly in generating optimized, coordinated multi-robot motion sequences that minimize the assembly duration. This work presents a simulation-based method for generating such optimized sequences. The approach separates assembly steps into task-related core operations and connecting traverse operations. While core operations are constrained and predetermined, traverse operations offer substantial optimization potential. Scheduling the core operations is formulated as an optimization problem, requiring feasible traverse operations to be integrated using a decomposition-based motion planning strategy. Several solution techniques are explored, including a sampling heuristic, tree-based search and gradient-free optimization. For motion planning, a decomposition method is proposed that identifies specific areas in the schedule, which can be solved independently with modified centralized path planning algorithms. The proposed method generates efficient and collision-free multi-robot assembly procedures that outperform a baseline relying on decentralized, robot-individual motion planning. Its effectiveness is demonstrated through simulation experiments.

</details>


### [393] [TRUST-Planner: Topology-guided Robust Trajectory Planner for AAVs with Uncertain Obstacle Spatial-temporal Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14610)
*Junzhi Li,Teng Long,Jingliang Sun,Jianxin Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRUST-Planner是一种拓扑引导的分层规划框架，通过DEV-PRM前端路径探索、UTF-MINCO与DDF后端轨迹优化，以及增量式多分支轨迹管理，实现了复杂动态环境下自主飞行器的高鲁棒时空避障，仿真成功率达96%且具备毫秒级计算效率，实物实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主飞行器运动规划框架在复杂动态环境中面临局部极小值和死锁问题，导致碰撞风险增加。

Method: 提出TRUST-Planner拓扑引导分层规划框架：前端采用动态增强可视概率路线图(DEV-PRM)快速探索拓扑路径；后端使用均匀无终端约束最小控制多项式(UTF-MINCO)和动态距离场(DDF)实现高效预测避障和并行计算；引入增量式多分支轨迹管理框架实现时空拓扑决策并利用历史信息减少重规划时间。

Result: 仿真实验中达到96%的成功率和毫秒级计算效率，实物实验验证了方法的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: TRUST-Planner有效解决了复杂动态环境下的局部极小值和死锁问题，为自主飞行器提供了鲁棒的时空避障能力。

Abstract: Despite extensive developments in motion planning of autonomous aerial vehicles (AAVs), existing frameworks faces the challenges of local minima and deadlock in complex dynamic environments, leading to increased collision risks. To address these challenges, we present TRUST-Planner, a topology-guided hierarchical planning framework for robust spatial-temporal obstacle avoidance. In the frontend, a dynamic enhanced visible probabilistic roadmap (DEV-PRM) is proposed to rapidly explore topological paths for global guidance. The backend utilizes a uniform terminal-free minimum control polynomial (UTF-MINCO) and dynamic distance field (DDF) to enable efficient predictive obstacle avoidance and fast parallel computation. Furthermore, an incremental multi-branch trajectory management framework is introduced to enable spatio-temporal topological decision-making, while efficiently leveraging historical information to reduce replanning time. Simulation results show that TRUST-Planner outperforms baseline competitors, achieving a 96\% success rate and millisecond-level computation efficiency in tested complex environments. Real-world experiments further validate the feasibility and practicality of the proposed method.

</details>


### [394] [Contrastive Representation Regularization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01711)
*Taeyoung Kim,Jimin Lee,Myungkyu Koo,Dongyoung Kim,Kyungmin Lee,Changyeon Kim,Younggyo Seo,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出机器人状态感知对比损失（RS-CL），一种针对VLA模型的轻量级表征正则化方法，通过将视觉-语言表征与机器人本体感知状态对齐，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率，在RoboCasa-Kitchen基准上达到69.7%的SOTA性能，并将真实机器人操作成功率从45.0%提升至58.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型虽利用了预训练VLM的丰富表征，但这些表征对机器人特有的信号（如控制动作和本体感知信息）不够敏感，导致表征质量次优，限制了操作性能。

Method: 提出RS-CL，利用机器人本体感知状态之间的相对距离作为软监督信号，将VLM的表征与机器人状态进行对齐，作为原始动作预测目标的补充正则化项，轻量且兼容标准VLA训练流程。

Result: 在RoboCasa-Kitchen基准上达到69.7%的SOTA性能；在具有挑战性的真实机器人操作任务中，成功率从45.0%提升至58.3%。

Conclusion: RS-CL是一种简单有效的表征正则化方法，能够有效弥合VLM表征与机器人信号之间的差距，提升VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in robot manipulation by leveraging rich representations from pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, their representations arguably remain suboptimal, lacking sensitivity to robotic signals such as control actions and proprioceptive information. To address the issue, we introduce Robot State-aware Contrastive Loss (RS-CL), a simple and effective representation regularization for VLA models, designed to bridge the gap between VLM representations and robotic signals. In particular, RS-CL aligns the representations more closely with the robot's proprioceptive states by using relative distances between the states as soft supervision. Complementing the original action prediction objective, RS-CL enhances control-relevant representation learning, while being lightweight and fully compatible with standard VLA training pipelines. Our empirical results demonstrate that RS-CL substantially improves the performance of state-of-the-art VLA models; it pushes the prior art to 69.7% achieving the state-of-the-art performance on the RoboCasa-Kitchen benchmark, and boosts success rates from 45.0% to 58.3% on challenging real-robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [395] [ScheduleStream: Temporal Planning with Samplers for GPU-Accelerated Multi-Arm Task and Motion Planning & Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04758)
*Caelan Garrett,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ScheduleStream，首个支持采样操作的通用规划与调度框架，通过混合持续动作和GPU加速采样器，将任务与运动规划(TAMP)扩展至双臂并行调度场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂和类人机器人的多臂并行控制虽具吸引力，但混合离散-连续动作空间带来的计算复杂性导致传统TAMP算法只能生成单臂串行计划，无法利用并行性实现高效任务调度。

Method: 提出ScheduleStream框架，采用混合持续动作（可异步启动、持续时间由参数决定）建模时序动态；设计领域无关的求解算法；在任务与运动规划调度(TAMPAS)中引入GPU加速采样器提升规划效率。

Result: 在仿真实验中与多种消融方法对比，ScheduleStream算法能生成更高效的并行调度方案；在多个真实双臂机器人任务上进行了实物验证。

Conclusion: ScheduleStream是首个支持采样操作的通用规划与调度框架，成功将TAMP扩展至双臂并行调度，在仿真和真实实验中均表现优于基线方法。

Abstract: Bimanual and humanoid robots are appealing because of their human-like ability to leverage multiple arms to efficiently complete tasks. However, controlling multiple arms at once is computationally challenging due to the growth in the hybrid discrete-continuous action space. Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) algorithms can efficiently plan in hybrid spaces but generally produce plans, where only one arm is moving at a time, rather than schedules that allow for parallel arm motion. In order to extend TAMP to produce schedules, we present ScheduleStream, the first general-purpose framework for planning & scheduling with sampling operations. ScheduleStream models temporal dynamics using hybrid durative actions, which can be started asynchronously and persist for a duration that's a function of their parameters. We propose domain-independent algorithms that solve ScheduleStream problems without any application-specific mechanisms. We apply ScheduleStream to Task and Motion Planning & Scheduling (TAMPAS), where we use GPU acceleration within samplers to expedite planning. We compare ScheduleStream algorithms to several ablations in simulation and find that they produce more efficient solutions. We demonstrate ScheduleStream on several real-world bimanual robot tasks at https://schedulestream.github.io.

</details>


### [396] [A Review of Learning-Based Motion Planning: Toward a Data-Driven Optimal Control Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11944)
*Jia Hu,Yang Chang,Haoran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了数据驱动最优控制（DDOC）范式在自动驾驶运动规划中的应用，该范式融合了最优控制的理论保证与机器学习的自适应能力，并提出了包含定制化、动力学自适应和自调谐三个关键维度的实现路线图，最后指出了弥合现实差距的四个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的规划方法可验证但泛化能力差，而基于学习的方法适应性强但存在不透明性和安全风险。现有综述通常孤立分析各类AI方法，忽略了将其与严格控制框架整合的潜力，因此需要弥合这一研究空白。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，首次从数据驱动最优控制（DDOC）范式角度审视自动驾驶运动规划，并将实现结构化为三个关键维度：定制化（customization）、动力学自适应（dynamics adaptation）和自调谐（self-tuning）。

Result: 提出了DDOC-based运动规划的第一个系统路线图，明确了三个关键实施维度，并识别出四个未来研究方向以弥合现实差距。

Conclusion: DDOC范式能够协同最优控制的理论保证与机器学习的自适应能力，是加速实现可信赖且类人自动驾驶的有效途径，但仍需进一步研究以弥合现实差距。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous driving (AD) faces a critical trade-off. While traditional rule-based pipelines offer verifiable safety and interpretability, they often fail to generalize in complex scenarios. Conversely, emerging learning-based methods-including imitation learning (IL), reinforcement learning (RL), and generative AI-offer greater adaptability but are often constrained by opacity and safety risks. Existing surveys typically analyze these AI methods in isolation, overlooking the potential of integrating them with rigorous control frameworks. To bridge this gap, this paper presents the first systematic review of the Data-Driven Optimal Control (DDOC) paradigm, explicitly examining how it synergizes the theoretical guarantees of optimal control with the adaptive capabilities of modern machine learning. Building on this framework, we propose the first roadmap for DDOC-based motion planning, structuring its implementation into three critical dimensions: customization, dynamics adaptation, and self-tuning. Finally, to close the remaining reality gap, we identify four future research directions, thereby accelerating the transition to trustworthy and human-like autonomous driving.

</details>


### [397] [Multifingered force-aware control for humanoid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08142)
*Pasquale Marra,Gabriele M. Caddeo,Ugo Pattacini,Lorenzo Natale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多指手机器人的力感知控制与力分配方法，利用触觉传感器估计力，设计模型控制方案最小化压力中心与指尖接触多边形质心的距离，在物体平衡任务中成功率达82.7%，多物体场景准确率达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多指手机器人在抓取质量分布变化或不稳定接触物体时，如何自适应调整躯干、手臂、手腕和手指运动以维持稳定接触的问题。

Method: 1) 使用五个Xela磁传感器与压头交互采集触觉信号与真实力测量数据集，训练力估计器；2) 提出基于模型的控制器，最小化压力中心(CoP)与指尖接触多边形质心之间的距离；3) 利用估计力而非原始触觉信号进行控制，使方法可迁移至其他力估计传感器。

Result: 在五种物体的平衡任务中实现82.7%成功率，在多物体场景中实现80%准确率。

Conclusion: 所提出的力感知控制框架能够有效适应不同质量分布或不稳定接触的物体，且由于依赖估计力而非原始触觉信号，具有良好的传感器通用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we address force-aware control and force distribution in robotic platforms with multi-fingered hands. Given a target goal and force estimates from tactile sensors, we design a controller that adapts the motion of the torso, arm, wrist, and fingers, redistributing forces to maintain stable contact with objects of varying mass distribution or unstable contacts. To estimate forces, we collect a dataset of tactile signals and ground-truth force measurements using five Xela magnetic sensors interacting with indenters, and train force estimators. We then introduce a model-based control scheme that minimizes the distance between the Center of Pressure (CoP) and the centroid of the fingertips contact polygon. Since our method relies on estimated forces rather than raw tactile signals, it has the potential to be applied to any sensor capable of force estimation. We validate our framework on a balancing task with five objects, achieving a $82.7\%$ success rate, and further evaluate it in multi-object scenarios, achieving $80\%$ accuracy. Code and data can be found here https://github.com/hsp-iit/multifingered-force-aware-control.

</details>


### [398] [VLA-ATTC: Adaptive Test-Time Compute for VLA Models with Relative Action Critic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01194)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Xiaobo Xia,Shan You,Yi Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ATTC通过不确定性驱动的"认知离合器"让VLA模型在快速直觉决策与自适应测试时计算推演之间动态切换，利用相对动作评论家(RAC)模型通过成对比较选择最优动作，在LIBERO-LONG基准上将SOTA模型PI0.5的失败率降低超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖快速直觉决策过程，缺乏推演能力，在复杂或模糊场景中容易做出次优甚至灾难性决策。

Method: 提出VLA-ATTC框架：1) 基于不确定性的"认知离合器"动态决定是否从直觉执行切换到TTC推演阶段；2) 相对动作评论家(RAC)模型通过成对比较从生成候选动作中识别最优动作，用相对排序替代不稳定的绝对值估计；3) 高效采样策略分摊计算成本；4) 自动化数据管道无需人工标注即可构建偏好对。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG基准上，VLA-ATTC将SOTA模型PI0.5的失败率降低超过50%。

Conclusion: 自适应测试时计算能有效增强VLA模型的推演能力，相对比较机制比绝对值估计更稳定且易学习，可大幅提升复杂任务的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities and generalization in embodied manipulation. However, their decision-making relies on a fast, instinctive process that lacks deliberation. This strategy often leads to suboptimal or catastrophic actions when facing complex or ambiguous scenarios that require greater consideration. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{VLA-ATTC}, a framework that endows VLA models with adaptive test-time compute (TTC). VLA-ATTC employs an uncertainty-based ``cognitive clutch'' to dynamically transition from reflexive execution to a TTC deliberation phase when necessary. During TTC phase, a novel \textbf{Relative Action Critic} (RAC) model identifies the optimal action from generated candidates via pairwise comparisons. This relative mechanism replaces unstable absolute value estimation, significantly simplifying the learning objective. Furthermore, we introduce an efficient sampling strategy to amortize computational costs and an automated data pipeline that curates preference pairs without manual annotation. On the LIBERO-LONG benchmark, VLA-ATTC reduces the failure rate of the SOTA model PI0.5 by over 50\%. We will open-source all the code and weights.

</details>


### [399] [HumanEgo: Zero-Shot Robot Learning from Minutes of Human Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24934)
*Zhi Wang,Botao He,Kelin Yu,Seungjae Lee,Ruohan Gao,Furong Huang,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanEgo是一种无需机器人数据、与硬件无关、数据高效的框架，通过将人类演示视频提升到实体级别的手-物交互表示，并训练具有密集辅助目标的流匹配策略，实现了零样本的人到机器人技能迁移。仅需30分钟人类视频即可达到92.5%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类第一人称视频包含了丰富的操作演示，但由于人类与机器人在视觉外观和运动学上的具身差距，将这些技能迁移到机器人上仍然充满挑战。

Method: 将每个人类演示提升到实体级别的手-物交互表示，并训练具有密集辅助目标的流匹配策略（flow matching policy），以放大每条轨迹的监督信号。

Result: 每项任务仅需30分钟人类视频，在四个真实世界任务中达到92.5%的平均成功率（15分钟视频可达75%），比同等时间的机器人遥操作高出41%，并能零样本鲁棒地迁移到新型机器人、摄像头和环境中。

Conclusion: HumanEgo是一个易用、开源的框架，可直接从人类数据学习机器人策略，无需机器人数据即可实现高效迁移。

Abstract: Human egocentric video captures rich manipulation demonstrations without any robot hardware, yet transferring these skills to robots remains challenging due to the embodiment gap between human and robot in both visual appearance and kinematics. We present HumanEgo, a framework that bridges the embodiment gap by lifting each human demonstration to an entity-level representation of hand-object interaction, and training a flow matching policy with dense auxiliary objectives that amplify supervision from every trajectory. HumanEgo is robot-data-free, hardware-agnostic, data-efficient, and zero-shot human-to-robot transferable. With only 30 minutes of human videos per task, HumanEgo achieves 92.5% average success across four real-world tasks (75% with just 15 minutes), outperforms matched-time robot teleoperation by 41%, and robustly transfers zero-shot across novel robots, cameras, and environments. We release HumanEgo as an easy-to-use, open-source framework for learning robot policies directly from human data: https://github.com/TX-Leo/HumanEgo

</details>


### [400] [When Should a Robot Think? Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Embodied Robotic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16673)
*Jun Liu,Pu Zhao,Zhenglun Kong,Xuan Shen,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Lin Cui,Hao Tang,Geng Yuan,Wei Niu,Wenbin Zhang,Xue Lin,Gaowen Liu,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RARRL框架，通过强化学习学习高层编排策略，使具身智能体能够自适应地决定是否调用推理、选择推理角色以及分配计算预算，从而在提升任务成功率的同时降低执行延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人系统依赖LLM智能体进行推理时面临计算延迟与资源开销问题：过度推理会延迟行动执行，而推理不足则导致错误决策和任务失败。核心问题在于智能体何时应推理、何时应行动。

Method: 提出RARRL（Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning）分层框架，不学习底层控制策略，而是通过强化学习学习高层编排策略，基于当前观测、执行历史和剩余资源，自适应决定是否调用推理、选择推理角色以及分配计算预算。

Result: 基于ALFRED基准的实证延迟评估实验表明，与固定或启发式推理策略相比，RARRL在降低执行延迟的同时持续提升任务成功率，并增强了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应推理控制对于构建可靠且高效的具身机器人智能体至关重要。

Abstract: Embodied robotic systems increasingly rely on large language model (LLM)-based agents to support high-level reasoning, planning, and decision-making during interactions with the environment. However, invoking LLM reasoning introduces substantial computational latency and resource overhead, which can interrupt action execution and reduce system reliability. Excessive reasoning may delay actions, while insufficient reasoning often leads to incorrect decisions and task failures. This raises a fundamental question for embodied agents: when should the agent reason, and when should it act? In this work, we propose RARRL (Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning), a hierarchical framework for resource-aware orchestration of embodied agents. Rather than learning low-level control policies, RARRL learns a high-level orchestration policy that operates at the agent's decision-making layer. This policy enables the agent to adaptively determine whether to invoke reasoning, which reasoning role to employ, and how much computational budget to allocate based on current observations, execution history, and remaining resources. Extensive experiments, including evaluations with empirical latency profiles derived from the ALFRED benchmark, show that RARRL consistently improves task success rates while reducing execution latency and enhancing robustness compared with fixed or heuristic reasoning strategies. These results demonstrate that adaptive reasoning control is essential for building reliable and efficient embodied robotic agents.

</details>


### [401] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA提出了一种基于语义接触上下文的元适应框架，用紧凑的6D接触上下文替代原始模拟器参数适应，通过可部署的因果Transformer在线推断接触语义，在力主导的装配任务中实现了无需演示、特权信息或梯度更新的片内适应，并在仿真和真实机器人上取得了优于FORGE基线的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMA方法在力主导的装配任务中表现不佳，FORGE基线在仿真中成功率高但在真实硬件上性能大幅下降，需要一种更鲁棒、更可迁移的适应方法。

Method: 提出CoRMA框架：(1) 用紧凑的6D模拟器专属语义接触上下文（接触起始、侧向啮合、导向过渡、接触方向、卡滞）替代原始模拟器参数适应；(2) 部署时使用因果Transformer从力、本体感觉和动作历史中通过语义回归和力模态对比目标推断该上下文；(3) 训练时使用oracle上下文，部署时替换为推断的上下文。

Result: 在PegInsert、GearMesh、NutThread三个任务上的仿真（Isaac Lab/Isaac Sim 5.0）和真实Marvin机械臂实验中，CoRMA在受控目标位姿噪声下相比FORGE基线取得了更高的真实验证成功率。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断可作为相关装配任务家族中的可复用适应接口，但跨未见任务的泛化和Real2Sim校准仍有待未来工作。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim 5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [402] [Scalable AI-Driven Analytics for User Engagement and Stance Detection on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29199)
*Thammitage Piyumi Wathsala Seneviratne,Muhammad Ikram,Dinusha Vatsalan,Hassan Asghar,Mohamed Ali Kaafar*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可扩展的AI驱动服务框架，用于分析用户对社交媒体内容（特别是阴谋论内容）的参与度和立场。框架集成了数据摄取、过滤、主题建模、情感分析和立场检测模块，在包含700万条评论、5万个YouTube视频的数据集上验证了有效性。研究发现阴谋内容在一周内吸引高达70%的用户参与，多数用户对阴谋叙事持支持态度，并识别出高度活跃的用户子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体已成为虚假信息和阴谋内容大规模传播的主要渠道，对公众信任、健康和社会稳定构成重大风险。以往研究主要从行为或内容中心视角分析此类内容，缺乏可扩展的、面向服务的解决方案来实现平台规模的用户参与度持续监控与分析。

Method: 提出一个可扩展的AI驱动服务框架，采用模块化流水线设计，包含数据摄取、过滤、主题建模、情感分析和立场检测等模块，能够在大型真实世界数据集上运行。

Result: 1）阴谋内容在发布第一周内吸引高达70%的总用户参与度，表现出强烈的早期放大动态；2）识别出一批高度活跃的用户，他们在多个视频和频道中表现出不成比例的高参与度；3）立场分析显示大多数用户对阴谋叙事持支持态度。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了部署可扩展的、面向服务的分析系统用于实时监控用户参与和行为模式的可行性，强调了早期检测和基于服务的监控对减轻有害内容传播的重要性。

Abstract: Social media platforms have become a major vector for the large-scale dissemination of misinformation and conspiracy content, posing significant risks to public trust, health, and societal stability. While prior work has primarily focused on analysing such content from a behavioural or content-centric perspective, there is a lack of scalable, service-oriented solutions that enable continuous monitoring and analysis of user engagement at platform scale.
  In this paper, we present a scalable AI-driven service framework for analysing user engagement and stance on social media content. Our system integrates data ingestion, filtering, topic modelling, sentiment analysis, and stance detection into a modular pipeline that can operate on large-scale, real-world datasets. We implement and evaluate our framework on a dataset comprising over 7 million user comments collected from nearly 50,000 YouTube videos associated with conspiracy narratives.
  Our analysis reveals that conspiracy content attracts up to 70% of total user engagement within the first week of publication, indicating strong early amplification dynamics. Furthermore, we identify a subset of highly active users who exhibit disproportionately high engagement across multiple videos and channels. Stance analysis shows that a majority of users express favourable positions toward conspiracy narratives, highlighting the role of user communities in reinforcing such content.
  The proposed framework demonstrates the feasibility of deploying scalable, service-oriented analytics for real-time monitoring of user engagement and behavioural patterns. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework in capturing large-scale engagement dynamics and highlight the importance of early-stage detection and service-based monitoring for mitigating the spread of harmful content.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [403] [Political Neutrality as Balanced Approval: A Large-Scale Human Evaluation of AI Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28911)
*Jonathan Stray,David Zhai Yang,Steven Luo,Miu Nicole Takagi,Serina Chang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI政治中立性的新定义，并通过大规模用户研究（7,434名参与者，208,152次评估）构建了PARETO数据集。定义基于政治理论：针对争议性问题，AI应最大化跨对立群体的认可度并平衡群体间认可度。研究对20个美国争议议题进行分析，发现AI回答可以同时获得双方高认可度，且默认回答偏向自由派（除Grok外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益影响政治观点，定义和评估AI政治中立性成为紧迫问题。现有缺乏基于实证的严格定义和基准测试。

Method: 1) 提出基于政治理论的中立性定义：针对争议性问题，AI回答应最大化跨对立群体的认可度并平衡群体间认可度；2) 构建包含20个美国争议议题的基准，从Reddit获取提示词；3) 招募7,434名参与者进行208,152次AI回答评价。

Result: 1) AI回答可以同时获得双方高认可度；2) 默认回答中GPT、Gemini、Claude、Llama偏向自由派，Grok无明显偏向；3) 带有政治倾向的用户提示比中性提示更难回应。

Conclusion: 本研究为AI政治中立性提供了严格定义和基准测试，以及用于衡量进展的数据集PARETO。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly shape political views, defining and evaluating AI political neutrality is an urgent problem. Here, we propose a new definition of AI political neutrality and design a large-scale user study to test it, releasing a new dataset PARETO with 7,434 participants and 208,152 evaluations of AI responses. Our definition follows a simple principle grounded in political theory: when asked about a controversial issue, an AI model should generate responses that maximize approval across groups with opposing viewpoints, while balancing approval between groups. This definition allows empirical testing of whether an AI response is "neutral" and generalizes to any political context without pre-supposing a single left-right axis of division. We construct a benchmark of controversial U.S. issues, with prompts sourced from politically charged questions on Reddit and responses from frontier AI models, and recruit human participants to rate AI responses. Across all 20 issues, we find that it is possible for AI responses to achieve high rates of approval on both sides, even as those sides disagree strongly with each other on the substance of the issues. We also find that default responses lean liberal for GPT, Gemini, Claude, and Llama, but not Grok, and that user prompts with political charges are harder to respond to than neutral prompts. This work introduces a rigorous definition and benchmark of AI political neutrality, and a dataset to measure progress toward it.

</details>


### [404] [Does Distributed Training Undermine Compute Governance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29359)
*Robi Rahman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文评估了通过分布式训练规避计算治理的可行性，并提出了包括举报机制、芯片追踪、法务会计以及集群内存和计算阈值在内的应对措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算治理提案通常假设前沿AI训练需要大规模、可检测的计算集群，但分布式训练算法的进步可能使开发者能够利用分散的硬件聚合进行前沿训练，从而规避监管。

Method: 本文通过分析分布式训练技术的现状，评估开发者规避计算治理的可行性，并提出相应的反制措施。

Result: 研究发现，分布式训练确实可能被用于规避计算治理，需要设计专门针对分布式场景的监管机制。

Conclusion: 计算治理必须考虑分布式训练带来的规避风险，通过多层次的反制措施（举报、芯片追踪、法务会计、集群阈值）来检测和防止非法分布式训练。

Abstract: Compute governance proposals often rely on the assumption that frontier AI training requires large, detectable computing clusters. However, recent advances in distributed training algorithms could allow developers to conduct frontier-scale training on distributed agglomerations of hardware, rather than needing large datacenter facilities. Developers who prefer not to be constrained by regulations may structure their hardware in a manner that evades the registration and monitoring requirements associated with compute governance. Therefore, regulations must be designed to detect and prevent illicit distributed training operations. This paper evaluates the feasibility of such evasion and outlines recommended countermeasures, including whistleblowing, chip tracking, forensic accounting, and memory and compute thresholds for clusters.

</details>


### [405] [When Should AI Read the Room? Public Perceptions of Social Intelligence in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29938)
*Leena Mathur,Jenny T. Liang,Vasudha Varadarajan,Jimin Mun,Xuhui Zhou,Jana Schaich Borg,Yonatan Bisk,Louis-Philippe Morency,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过混合方法调查（N=200），研究公众对AI智能体社会智能的感知、接受度及其影响因素，发现参与者普遍依据可观察行为而非AI意图来评判社会智能，并存在支持他人使用多于自己使用的“支持-采纳鸿沟”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交智能AI智能体（Social-AI）在日常环境中日益普及，公众对这些智能体的社会智能感知及接受度尚缺乏系统研究，而这对决定AI应扮演的角色至关重要。

Method: 对美国成年人（N=200）进行混合方法调查，考察参与者对当前AI智能体社会智能的信念、与社交智能相关的能力、影响接受度的情境因素以及公众担忧。

Result: 参与者普遍报告遇到过自认为具有社会智能的AI智能体，且判断基于可观察行为而非AI意图；在可接受性判断中存在支持-采纳鸿沟（支持为他人使用远高于自己使用）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了外行人对社交AI的担忧，为AI治理中关于适当部署环境、智能体角色及最终用户风险提供了信息参考。

Abstract: AI researchers have been advancing socially intelligent AI agents (Social-AI) across embodiments, from chatbots to physical robots. As Social-AI is increasingly deployed in everyday settings, decisions about the roles these agents should play will depend on how laypeople perceive them. However, public perceptions of social intelligence in AI agents and the acceptability of these agents remain largely understudied. We present a mixed-methods survey of adults in the United States (N=200) that examines social intelligence as a perceived construct in AI agents. Our survey investigates the extent to which participants believe current AI agents have social intelligence, abilities of agents that participants associate with social intelligence, contextual factors influencing participant acceptance of Social-AI agents, and concerns participants hold about these technologies. Participants widely reported having already encountered AI agents they perceived as socially intelligent and grounded their judgments in observable behaviors, more than beliefs about AI agency or intent. We identified a support-adoption gap in acceptability judgments: participants supported the existence of Social-AI agents for others far more than for their own personal use. Our analysis uncovers layperson concerns about Social-AI, informing AI governance regarding appropriate deployment contexts, agent roles, and risks to end users.

</details>


### [406] [Generalizing a Highly Configurable Analytics Pipeline to Replicate and Support Educational Research Across Multiple Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30303)
*Yallen Bai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为A4L（AI增强学习架构）的模块化数据架构，用于收集、整合和分析来自教育AI助手的异构学习交互数据。通过三个跨领域案例研究，验证了该分析管道能够复用统一统计方法处理不同结构的数据集，并支持分析能力向新领域的可扩展迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习环境中部署的AI助手产生了大量异构交互数据，但缺乏统一的数据架构来系统性地收集、整合和分析这些数据，以生成可迁移的教学洞察并支持个性化学习与师生双向反馈。

Method: 采用模块化数据架构设计A4L数据分析管道，定义设计原则和开发流程以保持领域灵活性；通过三个不同在线学习场景（对应三个教育AI助手）的案例研究，验证统一统计分析方法在不同数据结构与教学情境中的可复用性，并展示分析能力从初始领域向新领域的扩展过程。

Result: 一组通用的统计分析方法能够一致地应用于三种结构各异、教学背景不同的数据集，并复现各领域的关键分析发现；为一个领域开发的分析能力可被扩展以支撑另一领域更丰富的分析任务，证明了管道的可扩展性。

Conclusion: A4L分析管道可作为未来教育AI助手数据分析的可复用基础设施，通过系统性地将分析能力扩展至新领域，为教育AI系统的设计与评估提供洞察基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence assistants deployed in online learning environments create new opportunities to collect large volumes of learner interaction data and generate insights to improve student outcomes. Architecture for AI-Augmented Learning (A4L) is a modular data architecture that enables the collection, integration, and analysis of learner interaction data from educational AI systems, supporting the generation of instructional insights that facilitate personalized learning and reinforce the bidirectional feedback loop between instructors and learners. This study examines the modular design of the A4L Data Analytics Pipeline, an extensible data infrastructure that enables the ingestion, processing, and analysis of heterogeneous datasets generated by educational AI assistants. We describe the design principles and development process used to extend the pipeline's analytical capabilities while preserving flexibility across domains. We evaluate the pipeline through case studies spanning three research domains corresponding to three educational AI assistants deployed in online learning environments at Georgia Tech. Results show that a common set of statistical analysis methods can be consistently applied across datasets with differing structures and instructional contexts, enabling the pipeline to reproduce key analytical findings across domains. We demonstrate how analytical capabilities initially developed for one domain can be extended to support richer analyses in another, illustrating the pipeline's extensibility. These findings suggest that the A4L Analytics Pipeline can serve as reusable infrastructure for analyzing data generated by future educational AI assistants. By enabling analytics that can be systematically extended to new domains, the pipeline provides a foundation for deriving insights that inform the design and evaluation of educational AI systems.

</details>


### [407] [Agent4Edu: Generating Learner Response Data by Generative Agents for Intelligent Education Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10332)
*Weibo Gao,Qi Liu,Linan Yue,Fangzhou Yao,Rui Lv,Zheng Zhang,Hao Wang,Zhenya Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Agent4Edu是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的个性化学习模拟器，通过生成式智能体（配备学习者档案、记忆和动作模块）来模拟学生行为，以解决个性化学习系统中离线指标与在线表现不一致的问题，支持与自适应测试等算法交互，实现多维评估与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化学习是智能教育系统中有前景的策略，旨在提升学习者的练习效率。然而，离线指标与在线表现之间的差异严重阻碍了其进展。Agent4Edu旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: 利用大语言模型（LLM）构建生成式智能体，包含学习者档案（基于真实响应数据初始化，捕捉练习风格和认知因素）、记忆模块（基于心理学理论记录练习事实和高层摘要，集成反思机制）和动作模块（支持习题理解、分析和响应生成）。智能体可与个性化学习算法（如计算机化自适应测试）交互。

Result: 通过全面评估，探索了Agent4Edu的优势与不足，重点分析了智能体与人类学习者在响应上的一致性和差异。

Conclusion: Agent4Edu为个性化学习算法提供了一种新的模拟评估方式，有助于缩小离线指标与在线性能之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Personalized learning represents a promising educational strategy within intelligent educational systems, aiming to enhance learners' practice efficiency. However, the discrepancy between offline metrics and online performance significantly impedes their progress. To address this challenge, we introduce Agent4Edu, a novel personalized learning simulator leveraging recent advancements in human intelligence through large language models (LLMs). Agent4Edu features LLM-powered generative agents equipped with learner profile, memory, and action modules tailored to personalized learning algorithms. The learner profiles are initialized using real-world response data, capturing practice styles and cognitive factors. Inspired by human psychology theory, the memory module records practice facts and high-level summaries, integrating reflection mechanisms. The action module supports various behaviors, including exercise understanding, analysis, and response generation. Each agent can interact with personalized learning algorithms, such as computerized adaptive testing, enabling a multifaceted evaluation and enhancement of customized services. Through a comprehensive assessment, we explore the strengths and weaknesses of Agent4Edu, emphasizing the consistency and discrepancies in responses between agents and human learners. The code, data, and appendix are publicly available at https://github.com/bigdata-ustc/Agent4Edu.

</details>


### [408] [Multi-Level Barriers to Generative AI Adoption Across Disciplines and Professional Roles in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27052)
*Jianhua Yang,Kerem Öge,Adrian von Mühlenen,Abdullah Bilal Akbulut,Tanya Suzanne Carey,Chidi Okorro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过对一所罗素集团大学272名学术和专业人员调查，运用多项逻辑回归、结构方程模型和语义聚类等多方法分析，发现非STEM学者主要面临伦理和文化障碍（学术诚信），而STEM和专业服务人员则更多遇到制度、治理和基础设施限制，表明GenAI采用障碍是结构性生产的，而非仅个体因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常将GenAI采用障碍归因于个体层面的感知有用性和易用性，但不同学科和机构角色间的结构性障碍差异尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 采用多方法调查分析，对272名学术和专业服务人员进行调查，综合运用多项逻辑回归（MLR）、结构方程模型（SEM）和开放式回答的语义聚类分析。

Result: 非STEM学者主要报告与学术诚信相关的伦理和文化障碍，而STEM和专业服务人员则更多地强调制度、治理和基础设施方面的限制。

Conclusion: GenAI采用障碍深深嵌入组织生态系统和认识论规范中，大学需超越通用培训，开发针对特定角色的治理和支持框架。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is rapidly reshaping higher education, yet barriers to its adoption across different disciplines and institutional roles remain underexplored. Existing literature frequently attributes adoption barriers to individual-level factors such as perceived usefulness and ease of use. This study instead investigates whether such barriers are structurally produced. Drawing on a multi-method survey analysis of 272 academic and professional services (PSs) staff at a Russell Group university, we examine how disciplinary contexts and institutional roles shape perceived barriers. By integrating multinomial logistic regression (MLR), structural equation modelling (SEM), and semantic clustering of open-ended responses, we move beyond descriptive accounts to provide a multi-level explanation of GenAI adoption. Our findings reveal clear, systematic differences: non-STEM academics primarily report ethical and cultural barriers related to academic integrity, whereas STEM and PSs staff disproportionately emphasize institutional, governance, and infrastructure constraints. We conclude that GenAI adoption barriers are deeply embedded in organizational ecosystems and epistemic norms, suggesting that universities must move beyond generalized training to develop role-specific governance and support frameworks.

</details>
